Lean certifies the positivity integers, the defect endpoint, the two explicit lower bounds, the short-factor corners, and the reduction of the nonsplit tail at primes at least 37 to one degree-eight majorant. | `BGS-TRACE-123` | **PROVED** | Square-free products of at least three primes lie above 3/4 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/squarefree_product_abel.md) | Every square-free odd modulus with at least three distinct prime factors has L(M)>3/4, and the distance to (1-e^{-2})/4 exceeds 1/2. The fixed kick tau=10^{-80} preserves L(U)>3/4 when there are at least four prime factors and N<=10^{39}, when N is a product of three distinct odd primes not divisible by 15 and N<=10^{39}, and when N=15r with r an odd prime at least 7 and N<=10^{26}. The values r=3 and r=5 make 15r divisible by a square, so they are outside this note. For 15r above 10^{26} the pure sum stays above 3/4 and the kick is not claimed. A prime square is not treated here. The modulus 315=3^2*5*7 is not square-free; TRACE-124 places every odd modulus, and 315 is short because its radical 105 is short. Semiprimes remain the dichotomy of TRACE-122. No density is proved. The kick at u_207, the signed off-diagonal sum, the limit, and every k>2 stay open. Values above 3/4 lie at distance greater than 1/2 from the CUE value, on the side away from (1-e^{-2})/4. Lean certifies the corner integers, the finite-table comparisons, and the three rational lower bounds against 82/105. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-124` | **PROVED** | Every odd modulus lies above 3/4 or below 5/7 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/odd_modulus_abel.md) | Every odd modulus N>=3 satisfies L(M)>3/4 or L(M)<5/7. The proof assembles prime powers from TRACE-121 Theorems 1 and 2, semiprimes from TRACE-122 Theorem 3, square-free products of at least three primes from TRACE-123 Theorem 1, and the remaining non-square-free moduli from the radical comparison and Sections 2-7. The modulus 315=3^2*5*7 is short because its radical 105 is short, so no separate expansion at 315 is required. The fixed kick tau=10^{-80} preserves L(U)>3/4 when 8t(N)<=3N and N<=10^{39}, including every odd N whose radical is strongly short and including N=45; when N<=10^{26} and N is a ratio-preserving tower whose pure margin exceeds 13/420, namely N=3*7^a for a>=2, an inert full-order tower with the exponent of 7 at least 2, an inert tower 7q^b with the exponent of 7 equal to 1 and 13<=q<=39, and 3*5^b*13 for b>=2; and when N=15r with r an odd prime at least 7 and N<=2*10^{26}. A split full-order tower does not occur, and a non-short product of 3 with a short prime does not occur. The kick is not claimed for a ratio-preserving ceiling tower: at 3*23 the gap 575/22594 is smaller than 13/420. The kick is not claimed for an inert exponent-one partner q>=40, for 15r above 2*10^{26}, or at u_207. No density is proved. The signed off-diagonal sum, the limit, and every k>2 stay open. Values above 3/4 lie at distance greater than 1/2 from the CUE value, on the side away from (1-e^{-2})/4. Lean certifies the closed integer comparisons, including the displayed fraction at inert partner 13; the other twenty-six inert rows are recomputed by Python and C++. It does not formalize the kernel expansion, the quadrant comparison, or the kick telescoping. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-125` | **PROVED** | Uniform Pell-orbit overlap of the fixed symbol | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/uniform_orbit_overlap.md) | For every odd integer n>=5, at N=u_n, the Fourier overlap gamma(r) of the fixed 32-mode symbol under C is zero for 1<=r<=n-1 except at r=1 and r=n-1, where it equals -7/2000000. The quadratic trace coefficient of the transported product is exactly n*1650797/4000000. The index n=5 is a finite modular scan of the middle lags; every odd n>=7 is the size, archimedean, and quadratic-form argument. This does not bound the third-order remainder, does not prove L(U)>3/4 at any new dimension, does not prove that the weighted off-diagonal sum tends to its CUE correction, and does not decide the limit. The pure cat map is already non-CUE by TRACE-117. This claim does not assert that Kurlberg-Rudnick degeneracies fail to be eigenvalues of the fixed-kick propagator; that sentence does not follow from FM-032 and is not part of the proof. Lean certifies integer comparisons only: the rational seeds, the lag-1 coefficient, the box enumeration at exponent 1, and the exhaustive scan at N=41. It does not formalize the Pell induction, the archimedean embedding, or the quadratic-form descent. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-126` | **PROVED** | Phase-averaged form factor and classical symmetry defects of the kicked cat map | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/interaction_phase_form_factor.md) | For every odd N>=3, every m>=1 and every real eta, the average of \|Tr U(eta,phi)^m\|^2 over independent uniform phases on the 16 mode pairs equals \|ker(S^m-I)\| + eta^2 D_2(N,m) + rho, with D_2 an explicit Gauss-sum coefficient of size at most 2 m^2 sigma \|ker\| and with sigma=66033/160000. With x=m B \|eta\| and B=184981/160000, the averaged remainder satisfies \|rho\| <= N^2 [(x^3/6)(2+x^3/6)+x^4/4]. The fixed-kick remainder satisfies \|r\| <= N^2 [(x^3/6)(2+x^3/6)+x^3+x^4/4]. At an odd prime p, with m the order of S, D_2=-m (sigma-sigma_0(p)) p^2, where sigma_0(p) is the energy of the symbol modes congruent to 0 modulo p: sigma_0(3)=81/64000, sigma_0(5)=1/320000, sigma_0(7)=81/200, and sigma_0(p)=0 for every prime p>=11. The values include D_2(3,4)=-1184949/80000, D_2(5,6)=-79239/1280, D_2(7,3)=-181251/160000 and D_2(41,5)=-111001473/32000. The fixed kick has linear coefficient -2 m \|ker\| Im Sigma, nonzero at N=11, m=3 by a cyclotomic witness. Parity and position-basis conjugation commute or reverse M_N. Both kick defects vanish at N=7, where A_7=(9/10) I, and both are positive for every other odd N. For 0<\|eta\|<pi/B the kicked propagator therefore fails both symmetries outside dimension 7. The stable defects are 663/1000000 for odd N>=19 and 66031/80000 for odd N>=79. This is an auxiliary phase average, not the fixed kick and not the signed off-diagonal sum. No revival limit is proved: a positive limit of tau^2 N^3 at times comparable to N lies outside the truncation. The Kurlberg-Rudnick moments are not inputs. N-dependent antiunitaries other than these two symmetries are not classified. Lean certifies the scaled Fourier table, the energy, the parity energy, the majorant, the unreduced reversal sum, the three zero-mode energies and the scalar identity A_7=(9/10) I. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-127` | **PROVED** | Diagonal random-phase form factor of the cat map at times one, two and three | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/diagonal_phase_form_factor.md) | For every N>=1 and every complex N by N matrix with all entries of modulus N^{-1/2}, if D is diagonal with independent uniform phases and U=DM, then E\|Tr U^m\|^2 <= m! for every m>=1, E\|Tr U\|^2=1, and E\|Tr U^2\|^2=(2N-1)/N. For the cat-map quantization at every odd N>=3, E\|Tr U_N^3\|^2=(6N^2-9N+4)/N^2, strictly between 1 and 6. On those dimensions \|ker(S^2-I)\|=1 and \|ker(S^3-I)\| equals 49 if 7 divides N and equals 1 otherwise. By the trace-closure lemma of BGS-TRACE-126 these are the pure squared traces. The diagonal-phase value is strictly above the pure value at time 2, strictly above it at time 3 when 7 does not divide N, and strictly below 6 while the pure value is 49 when 7 divides N. The kernel does not grow with the exponent of 7: at N=49 it is 49, not 2401. This is not the 16-phase ensemble of BGS-TRACE-126 and not the fixed kick. For every diagonal unitary D, the antiunitary Theta = D K_0 satisfies Theta^2 = I and Theta (D M_N) Theta^{-1} = (D M_N)^dagger. The orthogonal-class label is the heuristic attached to that antiunitary and is not a COE form factor. Similarity to Sigma_D = D^{1/2} M_N D^{1/2} does not assign the class. The time-three limit 6 equals 2*3 because a 3-cycle and its reversal contribute the same amplitude. The time-two limit is 2, not 4, because a 2-cycle equals its own reversal. The measure is Haar measure on an N-torus, not Haar measure on the circular orthogonal ensemble. The bound m! does not separate a revival once m! >= N^2. Nothing beyond that bound is claimed for m>=4. A 24-draw splitmix sample at eta=2, recomputed in Python and C++, has mean 44.632431403503 at N=7 time 3 and mean 47.417022055369 at N=31 time 5, where \|ker(S^5-I)\|=1. That sample is not the sixteen-phase expectation and not a CUE or COE limit. Lean decides the matrix factorizations, the numerator identity (5N-4)(N-1)=5N^2-9N+4, and the strict gaps. It does not formalize the path count. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-128` | **PROVED** | Short-time limits of the diagonal-phase form factor | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/diagonal_phase_short_time.md) | For every odd N>=3 and the symmetric cat-map quantization M_N, with D diagonal and Haar on the N-torus, E\|Tr (D M_N)\|^2=1 and E\|Tr (D M_N)^2\|^2=(2N-1)/N. For every integer m>=3, the absolute difference between E\|Tr (D M_N)^m\|^2 and 2m P(N,m)/N^m is at most m!(1+m(m-1))/N, where P is the falling factorial. The fixed-m limit is 2m. The same bound gives the limit along m(N)<=c log N/log log N for any fixed c<1, and it does not reach m<=N^alpha. For an odd prime p, the row-inverted matrix W_p with pi(0)=0 and pi(x)=x^{-1} is flat, and V_p=D W_p. At p=3, pi is the identity. For every odd prime and every m>=3, the absolute difference between E\|Tr V_p^m\|^2 and m P(p,m)/p^m is at most m!(3^(1/4) p^(-1/4)+(m(m-1)+2)/p). The fixed-m limit is m. The logarithmic window uses c<1/4 and does not reach m<=p^alpha. A second independent diagonal does not break time reversal, because conjugating with D2 gives D2(D1 M D2)D2^{-1}=(D2 D1)M. The square-root conjugation is false. The constants are not sharp. No Heisenberg-scale statement is proved. This is not the sixteen-phase ensemble and not the fixed kick. The measure is not Haar measure on COE or CUE. Lean decides the symmetric constants 42, 312 and 2520, the identity (6N^2-9N+4)-6(N-1)(N-2)=9N-8, the comparison 9N-8<=42N, the integer main terms at N=3,5,7, and the Kloosterman counts 36 and 90. It does not sum the complex form factor. Python and C++ check the occupation inequalities in floating point, including N=3,5,7 and the time-four value at N=49. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-129` | **PROVED** | Bessel form of the scalar-return orbit sum | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/scalar_return_bessel.md) | If S^m is congruent to I modulo an odd N, then M_N^m is the scalar (Tr M_N^m)/N and U^m equals that scalar times the product of the Egorov transports of the phased kick. Modes congruent to 0 modulo N contribute only a global phase, and sigma_0(N)=0 for every N>9. For every odd N, det(S-I)=-4, so S-I is invertible and the order-two coefficient at time one vanishes: E\|Tr U\|^2 = 1 plus the remainder of BGS-TRACE-126, inside the window B\|eta\|<=1. For a position-diagonal kick the classical orbit sum has phase average N^{-4} sum_{z,z'} prod_r J_0(2 eta \|c_r\| \|V_r(z)-V_r(z')\|). At an odd prime whose first rows of S^0 through S^{m-1} are pairwise distinct, each non-zero frequency has mean square m, and the degree-two jet is 1 - m sigma_diag eta^2, the same jet as exp(-m sigma_diag eta^2). The degree-four gap is the number of non-permutation solutions of U_a+U_b=U_c+U_d. That excess vanishes at the primes 7, 31, 41 and 47, and equals 120 at p=11, where the Bessel average at lambda=1 is about 1.23 times the exponential. The modes with displacement ell=1 have symbol energy 9/160000, and the modes with ell=-1 have the same energy, with 4000 c_{1,k}=(-3-4i) r(k). Neither statement evaluates E\|Tr U^m\|^2 for m>=2. The time-one value remains 1+rho_1 from BGS-TRACE-129. The samples at N=201 and N=203 are not used. It does not formalize the antiunitary. Python and C++ check that D^{1/2} M D^{1/2} is symmetric and that B_N is odd at N=3,5,7,11. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-131` | **PROVED** | Diagonal phase average for every hyperbolic cat map | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/hyperbolic_diagonal_form_factor.md) | For every S=[[alpha, beta],[gamma, delta]] in SL(2,Z) with \|tr\|>=3, and every odd N>=3 with gcd(beta,N)=1, the position-basis matrix with phase kappa(delta x^2-2xy+alpha y^2), kappa=-(2 beta)^{-1}, and prefactor N^{-1/2} is unitary and flat. It is symmetric if and only if alpha is congruent to delta modulo N. The matrices [[6,1],[5,1]] at N=5, [[1,1],[3,4]] at N=3, and [[0,1],[-1,-3]] at N=3 are symmetric even though the integer diagonals differ. Let E be diagonal with entries e_N(kappa(delta-alpha)x^2). Then M = E M^T E^{-1}. For every diagonal unitary D, Theta' = D E K_0 satisfies Theta'^2=I and Theta'(D M) Theta'^{-1}=(D M)^dagger. Theta' reduces to D K_0 precisely when alpha is congruent to delta modulo N. The orthogonal-class name is a heuristic for Theta', not a COE form factor. Egorov holds, and \|Tr M^m\|^2 equals \|ker(S^m-I)\|. The sequences u_n and v_n, and det(S^n-I)=2-u_n, depend only on the trace. For an odd prime p not dividing beta the kernel has order 1, p, or p^2 according as p does not divide 2-u_m, S^m is a nontrivial unipotent, or S^m=I. The identity test depends only on the trace when p does not divide beta. If p does not divide 2(t^2-4), the order divides p-(Delta/p). If p divides beta, the matrices [[-20,-9],[9,4]] and [[-17,-18],[1,1]] have kernels 9 and 3 modulo 3. The certificate checks kernel 49 at time 3 modulo 49 for the two trace-6 matrices [[3,4],[2,3]] and [[5,2],[2,1]] only. The Bessel path sum allows frequencies divisible by N. D_2 is half of g''(0), with g the phase-averaged squared trace, and it equals the Gauss sum in the note. The expectation is even in eta; the x^3 piece of the remainder majorant is an artifact of the estimate. At an identity revival D_2=-m(sigma-sigma_0)p^2, including -42025/8, -375/8, and -75/2 for frequencies {1,5} at p=5. This is not the all-order revival law and not a fixed-time limit. Lean decides Cassini at ten pairs, u_3(6)=198, the companion and determinant identities for the listed matrices, and the kernels of those reduced matrices. The products 42025 and 375 are integer arithmetic, not a formalization of D_2. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-132` | **PROVED** | Fixed-time limits for a general hyperbolic cat map | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/hyperbolic_short_time_limit.md) | For every hyperbolic S in SL(2,Z) and every odd N coprime to the upper-right entry beta, Haar diagonal phases satisfy \|E\|Tr U^m\|^2 - 2m P(N,m)/N^m\| <= m!(1+m(m-1))/N for m>=3, where P is the falling factorial. The fixed-time limit is 2m. The antiunitary Theta' = D E K_0 of TRACE-131 reverses D M for every such S, and it reduces to D K_0 when alpha is congruent to delta modulo N. The orthogonal-class name is a heuristic for Theta'. At time one the limit is 1. At time two it is 2, not 4. A finite cosine with eta=o(N^{-2/3}) has limit 1 along odd N with gcd(N, beta(2-u_m))=1, including all but finitely many primes. Along N divisible by 7 (e.g. 35, 77, 91, 707) the time-3 kernel has order at least 49, so the limit 1 fails when gcd(N, beta(2-u_m)) > 1. A fixed nonzero eta is not forced near 2m: at N=11 and x=1 the remainder majorant N^2*11/18 exceeds 6. If the frequencies {1,...,m} are present, lim eta to infinity equals the Haar value, and the subsequent limit in N is 2m. The opposite order is not identified. A second diagonal does not produce the value m. Row inversion on an odd prime not dividing beta has limit m. A momentum sine is not identified. The 2m and m counts are those of Tanner and of Pakonski-Zyczkowski-Kus; the general-S error constant is the extension proved here. Lean decides only the arithmetic 42, 312, 13+9=22, 22*18=11*36, and 121*11>6*18. It does not formalize the occupation bound. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-133` | **PROVED** | Twisted closure formula for the diagonal-kick cat map at every time | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/revival_closure_law.md) | For every odd N, every m>=1 and every diagonal D, with D_j=M_N^j D M_N^{-j}, Tr(D_0...D_{m-1}) = N sum over a in Lambda_m of e_N(beta(a)) prod_j dhat(a_j), where Lambda_m={a in (Z/N)^m: sum_j a_j S^j e_2 = 0}, beta(a)=-2 sum_{i<j} a_i a_j y_{j-i} and S^d=[[x_d,4y_d],[2y_d,x_d]]. \|Lambda_m\|=N^{m-2} for m>=2 and Lambda_1={0}. a lies in Lambda_m iff a(S)=0 mod N iff N divides a(lambda) in Z[sqrt2] (N odd); Z[lambda]=Z[2 sqrt2] has index 2 and is not used. If S^m=I mod N then M_N^m=cI with \|c\|=1, \|Tr M_N^m\|=N and Tr U^m=(Tr M_N^m/N) Tr(D_0...D_{m-1}). Deleting e_N(beta) gives exactly the classical orbit sum. The general kick sum_u g(u)T(u) has the same formula with the symplectic cocycle phase. The phase average E\|Tr(D_phi M)^m\|^2 = sum_{x,y} K(x) conj K(y) prod_r J_0(w_r\|V_r(x)-V_r(y)\|) is the standard Kluyver average and is recorded, not claimed as new. The formula is an explicit form, with the Pell cocycle, of an exact expansion of Boasman-Keating type and specialises the trace closure of BGS-TRACE-126 and the scalar return of BGS-TRACE-129. | `BGS-TRACE-134` | **PROVED** | Pointwise revival bound at the scalar return and its limit on the minimal-period sequence | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/revival_closure_law.md) | Diagonal kick only (ell=0 rows, sigma_d=13203/32000; the full-kick sigma=66033/160000 exceeds it by the ell=+-1 rows, 9/80000). Norm lemma: a polynomial of degree <L with coefficients <=A and p(S)=0 mod N is a multiple of x^2-6x+1 when A^2 lambda^L<=4N^2; runs separated by gaps >floor(2 log_lambda(A/2)) are separately relations with at least three terms. If S^t=I mod N, N>18 and 2<=A<N/2, then for every phase vector \|Tr U^t/Tr M_N^t - dhat(0)^t\| <= E_T+E_D+E_R with E_T=t tau_A e^{W(t-1)}, E_D=sum_{s>=s0} C(t,s) rho^s, E_R=(1+w)^t-1, w=(G_A+1)^2 rho^3/(1-(G_A+1)rho), and \|dhat(0)-J\|<=W^3 e^W/6. If N tends to infinity with eta^2 t -> theta and t=2 log_lambda N + o(sqrt t), the ratio tends to exp(-theta sigma_d/2) uniformly in the phases, so E\|Tr U^t\|^2/N^2 -> exp(-eta^2 t sigma_d). The hypothesis holds on the Bonechi-De Bievre minimal-period sequence used by Faure-Nonnenmacher-De Bievre: 7, 41, 239, 1393, 8119, 47321 (kappa=4) and 35, 1189, 40391 (kappa=32). The explicit bound gives no information at computable N: at lambda_rev=1/4 it is 12.6 at N=41 and first falls below 1 near N=10^191 (0.788 at t=501); at lambda_rev=1 it is 2.36 at N=10^19139. Monte Carlo at N<=8119 is consistent with the limit and is a diagnostic only. Nothing is claimed at returns kt with k>=2, for generic N, for the full kick or for the fixed kick. Lean decides the orders at 7, 41, 239, 1393, 35 and 1189, kappa at nine family members (minimality is not certified for 8119, 47321 and 40391), Cayley-Hamilton, the Pell shape, and two exhaustive norm-lemma boxes (N=41, L=3, \|c\|<=5, which contains no non-zero closure; N=239, L=4, \|c\|<=6). It does not formalize the analytic bound. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-135` | **PROVED** | Half-period parity reduction of the scalar-return trace | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/revival_closure_law.md) | If N is an odd prime and t=ord_N(S) is even, then S^{t/2}=-I mod N, M_N^{t/2}=cP with P the parity and \|c\|=1, and Tr U^t/Tr M_N^t = N^{-1} Tr(A P A P) with A=D_0...D_{t/2-1} for every diagonal kick. The same holds for composite N whenever S^{t/2}=-I (201, 401, 801); it fails at 203, 205 and 1001, where S^{t/2} is 57I, 124I and 274I. The parity structure of half-periods is classical (Hannay-Berry, Kurlberg-Rudnick); only the reduction is recorded. Python checks S^{t/2}=-I at all 54 odd primes below 400 with even order and the reduction at 61 and 101 in floating point. Lean decides S^{t/2}=-I and the orders 62 and 102 at 61 and 101. | `BGS-TRACE-140` | **PROVED** | Unitary short-time form factor for Haar momentum phases | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/momentum_phase_cue.md) | For an odd prime p not dividing beta or delta, with lambda = delta^{-1} not congruent to -1 modulo p, Haar position phases and Haar momentum phases give E\|Tr U\|^2 = 1 and \|E\|Tr U^2\|^2 - 2\| <= 20/p (at time two this uses lambda not congruent to -1, i.e. delta not congruent to -1 mod p; for delta = -1 the limits are 4 and 2m). For every m >= 3 the distance from E\|Tr U^m\|^2 to m P(p,m)/p^m is at most (m!)^2 (1+2m(m-1))/p. The fixed-m limit is m, and the same bound reaches m <= c log p / log log p for any fixed c < 1/2. It does not reach m <= p^alpha. Position conjugation reverses the momentum factor if and only if its eigenvalues are even under j -> -j, a Haar-null condition. An antiunitary that is not diagonal in the position basis is not classified. This is not the fixed kick, not the sixteen-phase ensemble, and not Haar measure on U(p). BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-141` | **PROVED** | Unitary moment hierarchy for Haar momentum phases | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/momentum_phase_cue.md) | Under the hypotheses of BGS-TRACE-140, for each fixed integer k >= 1 and every m >= 3 the distance from E\|Tr U^m\|^{2k} to k! m^k P(p,km)/p^{km} is at most ((km)!)^2 (1+2km(km-1))/p. The fixed-time limit is k! m^k. For fixed k the logarithmic window has c < 1/(2k) and does not reach m <= p^alpha. The measure is not Haar measure on U(p). The listed programs recompute Haar buckets and integer constants; they do not compute a 2k-th moment. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-142` | **VERIFICATION-PENDING** | Gaussian momentum-phase crossover from 2m to m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/momentum_phase_cue.md) | Under the hypotheses of BGS-TRACE-140, the Gaussian main terms are m rotations and m e^{-vm} from reflections; the non-injective remainder is not bounded, so the limit m(1+e^{-vm}) is verification-pending. The evaluation of those main terms inverts A and uses lambda^t not congruent to 1 for t from 1 through m; TRACE-140 itself uses only lambda not congruent to -1. At v=0 the proposed limit is 2m and as v tends to infinity the proposed limit is m. With tau = m/p and Lambda = v p the proposed limit equals p tau (1+e^{-Lambda tau}), the diagonal approximation of the Pandey-Mehta form factor. The odd part of the maintained symbol has energy 9/125000. Setting v equal to that energy times eta^2 produces Lambda = (9/125000) eta^2 p (a convention; partner-variance matching gives twice this). That identification is not an evaluation of the sixteen-phase propagator. The formula is not stated for m < 3. Encounter corrections of order tau^2 are not proved. A seeded sample of 600 draws at p=29, m=3, v=0.4 has mean 3.709. An independent exact value is 3.7555 at p=29, and Richardson extrapolations give 3.8985 against 3.9036 at v=0.4 and 3.144 against 3.149 at v=1. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-143` | **PROVED** | Haar diagonal-phase cat map equals the random-phase DFT star graph | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | For every odd N and S=[[3,4],[2,3]], M_N = Delta F_{1/4} Delta with Delta=diag e_N(-3*8^{-1}x^2), and P F_{1/4} P^{-1} = F (DFT) for (Pf)(x)=f(2x). For every theta, D_theta M_N is unitarily conjugate to D_theta' F with theta'_x = theta_{2x} - 2pi*6*8^{-1}(2x)^2/N, a measure-preserving bijection of the N-torus, so the eigenvalue law under Haar theta is that of Tanner's DFT star graph, which is also the class-AI star graph of Gnutzmann-Seif 2004. For a general unit cross coefficient beta the law depends only on the square class of beta. At prime N=p a non-square class flips the sign of every half-integer (sqrt p) term of E\|Tr U^m\|^2 and leaves every integer-order term unchanged; this matters only for p=1 mod 4 (p=13, m=4: 14033/2197 +/- 8 sqrt13/2197). Odd N only. The identification is elementary. Floating-point checks at N=5..103, including composite N, give errors <=5.4e-16, and eigenphases agree to <=4.1e-15. Not formalized in Lean. Not the fixed kick and not the 16-mode ensemble. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. Lean note (Lean batch 3, 1 November 2026): superseding "Not formalized in Lean" for these identities, the matrix identities of Theorem 1 (M_N = Delta F_{1/4} Delta, P F_{1/4} P^-1 = F and the unitary conjugacy of D_theta M_N to D_theta' F) are formalized in formalization/Bgs/LineCDFTStar.lean; the measure statement, the square-class remark and the numerics are not. |
| `BGS-TRACE-144` | **PROVED** | Exact Gauss-sum and partition-lattice formula for the Haar diagonal-phase form factor | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | For odd N, E\|Tr U^m\|^2 = N^{-m} sum_{rho in Pi_m} W(rho) sum_{sigma in S_m} G_N(rho,sigma), with G_N = sum_z e_N(c Q_{rho,sigma}(z)), c=4^{-1}, and W(rho) the product over blocks of w(\|B\|), where sum w(n)t^n/n! = log sum t^n/(n!)^2 (w = 1, -1/2, 2/3, -11/8, 19/5). At prime p, G_p = p^{n-r/2} eps_p^r (delta/p). E is rational for every p=3 mod 4, lies in Q(sqrt p) for p=1 mod 4, and has no p^{-1/2} term. By Hadamard every relevant minor is at most 4^m in absolute value, so for p>4^m the rank and the square class over F_p equal those over Q. Brute force with the literal M_N matches at 19 (N,m) cases including composite N (1e-13), and exactly in rationals at the referee's 17 (p,m) pairs. Lean decides the counting step at N=7,11,13, m=3 (235/49, 631/121, 901/169), not the probabilistic reduction. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-145` | **PROVED** | Complete classification of the first off-diagonal pairs: 2-opt, swap and figure-eight | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | At deficit 0, rank_Q(A_C - A_H)=2 for a Hamiltonian cycle H != C holds iff H is a 2-opt of C (m(m-3)/2 cycles) or a swap of two vertices at cyclic distance >=3 (m(m-5)/2 cycles, m>=5). Each gives a hyperbolic Gauss sum N^{n-1} for every odd N. The diagonal is D_m (2m permutations, m>=3). At deficit 1 and rank 0 the pairs are the figure-eight words, m d(g) d(m-g) of them, with weight w(2)=-1/2. Rank 1 is impossible at deficit 0. Ranks 1 and 3 are absent at deficit 0 for m<=9 here (m<=10 in the referee check), but that is not a general lemma. The swap class is absent from the three diagram families of Gnutzmann-Seif 2004. Exhaustive checks: Lemma 4 for m<=9 (m<=10 by the referee), Lemma 5 for m<=9. Lean decides m=6 only: aut6, ham6=60, rank2_class6, rank2_counts6 (1, 9, 3) and fig8_6. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-146` | **PROVED** | Exact tau^2 coefficient of the Haar diagonal-phase cat-map form factor at fixed time | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | For m>=3 and every prime p>4^m, E\|Tr(D M_p)^m\|^2 = sum_{k=0}^{2m-1} a_{m,k}(p) p^{-k/2} exactly. Each a_{m,k} is a Q-combination of eps_p^r (D/p) with r=k mod 2, so it vanishes for k odd and p=3 mod 4. The coefficients a_0=2m and a_1=0 hold for all m>=3; a_2=-2m^2 for every m>=5, with -9 at m=3 and -24 at m=4. The values at m=1,2,3 (1, 2-1/N, 6-9/N+4/N^2) are BGS-TRACE-127 and are not re-claimed; the m=3 coefficient is a consistency check. This is the COE coefficient -2 tau^2: 2-opt pairs give m^2(m-3), swaps m^2(m-5) and figure-eights -2m^3+6m^2. Corollary: \|E - 2m - a_2/p\| <= C_m p^{-3/2} with C_m = m! sum\|W\| (3651, 90921, 3.1e6, 1.4e8 at m=5..8). That explicit bound is informative only for p > (C_m/2m^2)^2 (5.3e3 at m=5, 1.1e12 at m=8, 2.3e26 at m=12). Fixed m only, not uniform in m, nothing at fixed tau. The tau^2 coefficient was derived diagrammatically for the same ensemble, in the limit n,B->infinity, by Gnutzmann-Seif 2004 (PRE 69 056219/056220), and is predicted non-rigorously by Gnutzmann-Altland 2005 and Pluhar-Weidenmuller 2014. Exact all-pairs combinatorics for random-phase star graphs goes back to Berkolaiko-Keating 1999 (J. Phys. A 32 7827). What is new is the rigorous fixed-m, exact-in-p statement with a complete pair classification, including the swap class. Refines BGS-TRACE-128 at prime N. Lean decides coeff_identity for 5<=m<=300 and hyp_counts at p=5,7,11,13. It does not formalize the Gauss-sum evaluation or the Hadamard bound. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-147` | **VERIFICATION-PENDING** | Computer-assisted exact expansions to m=12 and the fixed-m tau^3 deviation from COE | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | Exact large-p expansions for p>4^m: all orders for m<=10, and through p^{-5/2} for m=11,12 (partitions of deficit<=2). There is no p^{-3/2} term for m<=12. c2 equals 2m^3 at m=7,10,11 and differs at m=5 (175+10(5/p)), 6 (390), 8 (1008), 9 (1512) and 12 (3480 vs 3456); \|c2-2m^3\|<=3.4m^2. This is a fixed-m deviation from the finite-N COE value. It does not contradict Gnutzmann-Seif, whose result stops at O(tau^3). In K at fixed tau it is (c2-2m^3)/m^2 * tau^2/p, consistent with COE in the scaling limit, but that limit is not proved. p^{-5/2} appears only for p=1 mod 4, nonzero at m=4,6,8,10 (8, -72, 128(2/p), 200) and zero at m=12. These values are for the square class of the cross coefficient, which holds for this S. Character terms appear at p^{-2} (m=5) and p^{-3}: (5/p), (2/p), (-3/p), (-7/p). COMPUTATIONAL: the expansion is exact at every prime 3<=p<=257 for m=2,3,4, at every prime 3<=p<=257 except p=5 for m=5, at every prime 7<=p<=257 for m=6,7 (it fails at 3 and 5), and at every prime 19<=p<=257 and at p=11 for m=8 (it fails at 3,5,7,13,17). The m=2,3 rows re-check BGS-TRACE-127, which holds for every odd N, and are not re-claimed. Exact arithmetic (C++ rank mod 2^61-1 under the Hadamard bound, Bareiss determinants, Python fractions). A second implementation exists for m<=8 in the packet. The referee's from-scratch engine is deposited at computations/verification/lineC/independent_replay/, and evidence/executions/20261029/lineC-random-phase/independent_replay/compare_theirs.log records that it reproduces every coefficient for m=3..12 at 171 primes in [101,1200], including m=11 and m=12 through p^{-5/2}. No Lean. The state remains verification-pending: the expansions and the small-prime ranges are computer-assisted, and promotion is a separate status decision, as for BGS-RES-022. BGS-TRACE-005 and BGS-BGS-001 remain open. |
| `BGS-TRACE-148` | **OPEN** | Scaling-limit conjecture for the Haar diagonal-phase cat-map form factor | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/random_phase_encounter_pairs.md) | Prove or refute Conjecture 8: c_2(m)=2m^3+O(m^2); for each k the trivial-character coefficient of p^{-k} is the COE leading term times (1+O(1/m)); and every half-integer or character term is O(m^k) at order p^{-k} or p^{-k-1/2}, hence vanishes in the tau-scaling limit. |
| `BGS-TRACE-149` | **PROVED** | Band-limited random kick: exact trace bridge E\|Tr U^m\|^2 = Psi_m(t_N) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | U = exp(i eta V_theta) M_N, M_N the S=[[3,4],[2,3]] cat-map matrix of Lines B/C, V_theta(x) = sum_{k in K} a_k cos(2 pi k x/N + theta_k), a fixed finite mode set K with a_k != 0; J0 ensemble: theta_k iid uniform; G ensemble: circular complex Gaussian coefficients with E\|c_k\|^2 = a_k^2. H_m = 6I - C - C^T, D_m = det H_m = Tr S^m - 2, X_m = D_m H_m^{-1}. For every odd N with gcd(N, D_m) = 1 (primality not used) and every theta: Tr U^m = Tr M_N^m sum_n Ghat_theta(n) e(t_N 2 n^T X_m n), t_N = (D_m^{-1} mod N)/N, \|Tr M_N^m\| = 1; hence E_theta\|Tr U^m\|^2 = Psi_m(t_N), Psi_m C^infinity (not claimed real-analytic), even (V -> -V symmetry), 1-periodic, independent of N. This is Line B Theorem 1 (Line B packet) applied for each theta; the mechanism is Boasman-Keating 1995 section 5 and is not claimed as new. When N \| D_m the normalisation fails. Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-150` | **PROVED** | Band-limited random kick: 1/N expansion and residue period g'_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | t_N = t* + 1/(N D_m), t* = -N^{-1}/D_m (mod 1); E = Psi_m(t*) + c_1/N + O(N^-2), c_1 = Psi_m'(t*)/D_m, for fixed (m, K, a, eta). Psi_m(t*) depends only on +-N mod g'_m, g'_m = D_m/gcd(D_m, 2X_ii, 4X_ij) = 14, 48, 82, 280, 478, 1632, 2786 (m=3..9), an upper bound on the period. The O(N^-2) constant is NOT uniform in eta or K; the limits N -> infinity and eta -> infinity do not commute (m=3, K=1, J0, p=101: p(E-Psi(t*)) = 40.5 vs c_1 = -201 at eta=16). The condition eta^2 (sum k^2 a_k^2) m << N is heuristic. Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-151` | **PROVED** | Band-limited random kick: orbit-pair formula and Gauss-weight support | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | Psi_m(s/D) = sum over xi, xi' in Fix_m = {xi : H_m xi in Z^m} (period-m solutions of q_{j+1}+q_{j-1} = 6 q_j mod 1; same count D_m as Fix S^m, no bijection claimed) of phihat_s(xi) conj phihat_s(xi') prod_k omega_k(eta(S_k(xi)-S_k(xi'))), omega = J0(a\|z\|) (standard Kluyver average, as in BGS-TRACE-133) or exp(-a^2\|z\|^2/4). \|phihat_s\|^2 in {0, \|V[4]\|/D_m}, support independent of s (Line B Prop R). D_eff = 49, 72, 1681, 4900, 57121 (m=3..7). Orbit form not claimed as new (Boasman-Keating). Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-152` | **PROVED** | Band-limited random kick: strong-kick limit B_m^sym at every residue under P_K-separation | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | If a_k != 0 for k in K and P_K(xi) = (S_k(xi))_{k in K} separates dihedral (Gamma) classes on the support, then lim_{eta->inf} Psi_m(t*) = B_m^sym = D_eff^{-1} sum_{supp}\|Gamma xi\| for every residue. Iterated limit (N -> infinity first). Quenched-ensemble analogue of Line B Theorem 4. At finite eta Psi_m(t*) is residue dependent and takes values in a range containing 2m; the COE value 2m is not obtained at any fixed m. Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-153` | **VERIFICATION-PENDING** | Band-limited random kick: computer-assisted instances m=3..7 (B^sym values, Gamma-separation, K=1 collisions) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | B_m^sym = 235/49, 61/12, 15801/1681, 27417/2450, 788019/57121 (m=3..7). Multiset => Gamma-class on Fix_m checked exactly for m <= 7, so K = {1..m} and K = {1,2} separate Gamma-classes for m <= 7. K = {1}: separates on the support for m = 3, 5, 7 (gaps 0.753, 1.936e-3, 3.685e-5); m = 4 and 6 have one collision group each (2 and 26 Gamma-classes, half-turn invariant; listed sequences Lean-certified as orbits, support/distinctness/completeness checked in Python). Non-separation is necessary, not sufficient, for residue dependence of the limit (m=4, K=1: 4.861111 at all residues; m=6, K=1: {10.705923, 11.165914}). eta-threshold tables are N -> infinity values only. Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-154` | **PROVED** | B_m^sym < 2m for every odd m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | For odd m, D_eff = a_m^2 with a_m odd, so the Gamma-invariant support has odd size; the reversal involution has a fixed point xi_0 with \|Gamma xi_0\| <= m, hence 2m - B_m^sym >= m/D_eff > 0. Same argument whenever 2m does not divide D_eff (e.g. m = 6). Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |

| `BGS-TRACE-157` | **PROVED** | 1/N coefficient: Gamma-diagonal part vanishes; oddness in the residue | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | c_1 = (2/pi) Im sum phihat(xi) conj phihat(xi') E[G(xi) conj G(xi') beta(xi)], beta = i eta h_0 sum v''(xi_i) - eta^2 v'^T H^{-1} v'. (a) The pairs xi' in Gamma xi contribute zero (both ensembles). (e) c_1(-N mod g'_m) = -c_1(N mod g'_m), so c_1 averages to 0 over residues and cannot equal -2m^2 uniformly in N; for single residues and some eta, c_1 = -2m^2 is not excluded. Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |
| `BGS-TRACE-158` | **CONDITIONAL** | 1/N coefficient, Gaussian ensemble: c_1 -> 0 as eta -> infinity under Gamma-separation | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/band_limited_kick_bridge.md) | G ensemble only. Tilted expectation f*b with f = prod exp(-a_k^2 eta^2 \|Delta S_k\|^2/4) and b real, polynomial of degree 4 in eta; if P_K separates Gamma-classes with gap delta, \|c_1\| <= C_m eta^4 exp(-a_min^2 eta^2 delta^2/4) -> 0. Separation holds for K containing {1..m} or {1,2} only for m <= 7 (computer check, BGS-TRACE-153). Non-separating K: c_1 grows (m=4, K=1: c_1 = +-0.302 eta^2). Does not prove BGS-TRACE-005 or BGS-BGS-001; says nothing about eigenvalue statistics beyond the second moment of Tr U^m at fixed m. |

With BGS-TRACE-146 (prime p > 4^m): E = 2m - 2m^2/p + O_m(p^{-3/2}) for m >= 5. Only a transfer of Line C, not a new COE theorem. G full band: sites iid up to a global phase that cancels. | `BGS-TRACE-163` | **PROVED** | Fixed diagonal kick: exact Pell-norm trace formula, orbit form and kernel structure; 2m law at m <= 8 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | U = K_eta M_N with K_eta = diag e^{i eta f(q/N)}, f the fixed real 32-mode trigonometric position kick, S=[[3,4],[2,3]], odd N with gcd(N,D_m)=1, fixed m. Theorem 1: Tr(K_eta M)^m = Tr M^m * sum_n Phihat(n) e(Q(n)/N) e(-nu_N y'_m Nm(alpha_n)/g'_m), nu_N = N^{-1} mod g'_m, alpha_n in Z[lambda], lambda=3+2sqrt2; hence Tr U^m / Tr M^m = Z_m(nu_N; eta) + O(1/N). Theorem 3 / Prop R: orbit form over the q-sequence group Fix(T^m), T=[[0,1],[-1,6]], with \|k_nu\|^2 in {0,\|V[4]\|/D}. Theorem 2: the N-average equals the nu-average F_m(eta). The eta-Bohr mean B_m equals 2m for m<=8 (m != 6 treated exactly, distinctness of S-classes certified by an exact cyclotomic class computation; F1, F2 wording). Time reversal U^T = K^{-1} U K is the alpha=delta case of BGS-TRACE-131 and is cited, not re-claimed. Pointwise self-averaging in one N is false (\|Tr U_N^m\|^2 is asymptotically a non-constant function of N^{-1} mod g'_m). Fixed m only; diagonal (l=0) kick only; nothing at tau = m/N = O(1). |
| `BGS-TRACE-164` | **CONDITIONAL** | 2m law for the eta-Bohr mean at general m, conditional on ND_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | Theorem 4: under ND_m, 0 <= 2m - B_m <= 224 m^2 D_m^{-1/2}. Theorem 5: for eta = tau N the N-average over D_eff/(delta_m tau) << X << tau^{-1-1/(m+1)} performs the eta-average; with tau = 1e-80 and the crude constants the window is non-empty only for m <= 6. ND_m is verified exactly for m <= 8 (m != 6, where two torsion coincidences are identified exactly) and by float clustering at m = 9. For general m the statement is conditional on ND_m; for the eleven certified NSW-prime m see BGS-TRACE-168. 2m is the COE diagonal-approximation value at fixed m (tau = m/N -> 0), not a statement at tau = O(1). B_m -> 2m is not claimed. |

Lemma S + Addendum (every odd m): D_m = 4 h_m^2, V_2 = <1> = Z/4, support S = 1/2*1 + V^_odd. Lemma F: reflections fix h_d period-d points. Theorem D: 2m - B^sym_m = h_m^{-2} sum_{d\|m}[2(m-d)a(d) + d^2 b(d)] = m^2/h_m (1+O(lambda^{-m/6})), uniformly in odd m. Concerns B^sym (Gamma-diagonal), not B_m itself. |
| `BGS-TRACE-166` | **PROVED** | p-splitting lemma for vanishing sums and symbol facts (C2, C3) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | C2: for p prime, p not dividing M, A: mu_{p^e} -> Q(zeta_M) with sum A(w) w = 0 is constant on mu_p-cosets; a vanishing sum supported on fewer than p points is zero. C3: Galois covariance S(a xi) = sigma_a S(xi) (a = 1 mod 4); the aliased symbol has affine symmetries only at n = 7 (exhaustive n <= 400; argument for n >= 19); shifted-symbol structure c~_{+-7} = (-360, 0) (sign fixed by the referee), separation 320 < 360. Specific to the fixed 32-mode kick. |
| `BGS-TRACE-167` | **PROVED** | Automatic admissibility for NSW-prime m (Frobenius argument) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | For odd m with h_m prime and h_m > 18: (a) lambda^d != k'/k mod h for all d and k != k' in the support K of the kick symbol, and (b) lambda^a + lambda^b = 6 only for {a,b} = {1,-1}. Argument (O/hO = F_h x F_h always splits, since 2 is a square mod h; sigma = conjugation, sigma(lambda) = lambda^{-1}; ord_h lambda = m): lambda^d in F_h forces lambda^d = +-1, hence = 1; lambda^a + lambda^b = 6 forces lambda^a lambda^b = 1, and of the four roots of t^2 - 6t + 1 in F_h x F_h only lambda, lambda^{-1} have norm 1, so {lambda^a,lambda^b} = {lambda,lambda^{-1}}. Not used by BGS-TRACE-168, whose eleven instances are checked directly. |
| `BGS-TRACE-168` | **PROVED** | Theorem N: unconditional formal non-degeneracy bound for the eleven certified NSW-prime m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | For m in {7, 19, 29, 47, 59, 163, 257, 421, 937, 947, 1493} (exactly the m for which h_m is certified prime; h_m > 28m; hypotheses (a), (b) verified for these m): every S-coincidence on the support is formal and \|B_m - B^sym_m\| <= 8 m^2 N_m / h_m, N_m = C(14m,2) + m + m(m(m+1)/2 - 1). The bound is < 1 only from m = 29 on (10^{3.91} at m = 7, 12 at m = 19). With BGS-TRACE-165 this gives \|B_m/2m - 1\| explicitly small for these m. It concerns the eta-Bohr mean B_m, not F_m(eta) at fixed eta. The infinitude of NSW primes is open, so B_m -> 2m is not claimed. |
| `BGS-TRACE-169` | **PROVED** | Proposition C: norm-class formula for the fixed-eta second moment, all odd m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | For odd m, h = h_m, q(x) = x0^2 - 6 x0 x1 + x1^2 mod h, A_r(eta) = sum_{q(x)=r} e^{i eta S(1/2 + x/h)}: F_m(eta) = h^{-2} sum_{r,r'} A_r conj(A_r') mu(q_rr')/phi(q_rr'), q_rr' = h/gcd(r-r',h). Self-duality with norm-torus Kloosterman sums (orthogonality for prime h). Fixed-kick counterpart of the random-phase Gauss-sum formula (BGS-TRACE-144), proved independently. |
| `BGS-TRACE-170` | **PROVED** | Theorem W (parity decomposition) and Theorem T (transfer-operator formula for chi_m) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | Theorem W: for odd m and every eta, F_m(eta) = B^sym_m + 2m chi_m(eta) + W~rest_m(eta), W~rest := W^rest + E, \|E\| <= 4m^2/h_m, chi_m(eta) = h^{-2} sum_{x in V^_odd} exp(i eta [S(1/2+x/h) - S(1/2-x/h)]). Theorem T: chi_m(eta) = sum_{a=0}^{3} (-1)^a Tr M_a(eta)^m with M_a[(p,q),(q,r)] = what_eta(6q - p - r + a), absolutely convergent, explicit truncation error. Identities only: no bound on W~rest is claimed. |
| `BGS-TRACE-171` | **PROVED** | Lemma V and Proposition L: lower bound chi_m(eta) >= 1 - m sigma^2 eta^2 / 2 for odd m >= 5 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | Lemma V: for odd m >= 5 no nonzero n in H Z^m + Z 1 has \|supp n\| <= 2 and \|n\|_inf <= 18 (norm argument h^2 \| Nm(a + b lambda^d) for m >= 9; finite check for m = 5, 7). Prop L: E X = 0, E X^2 = m sigma^2 (sigma^2 = 3.75e-4) and chi_m(eta) >= 1 - m sigma^2 eta^2/2 for all real eta. Fails at m = 3 (7 e_0 is a relation; 7 = h_3). Informative only for m << 1/(sigma^2 eta^2). |
| `BGS-TRACE-172` | **PROVED** | Tilted majorant, cycle inequality and Collatz-Wielandt trace bound for the parity transfer operator | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | With eigen-coordinates along lambda, lambda^{-1}: gamma0 sum N(s_j) <= c sum \|n_j - a\| around every closed cycle (gamma0 = 2sqrt2 - 2, c = 1/(2sqrt2)); hence \|Tr M_a(eta)^m\| <= Tr A_a^m for the tilted majorant A_a and all eta in an interval, and Tr A^m <= rho^{m-1} sum_s (Av)(s)/v(s) whenever v > 0 and Av <= rho v on Z^2. Framework only; the numbers are BGS-TRACE-173. |
| `BGS-TRACE-173` | **PROVED** | Theorem D_cert: certified exponential decay of chi_m on eta in [1, 81.574] | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | For every eta in [1, 81.57406988] (93 closed intervals I with shared endpoints) and every odd m >= 5: 1 - m sigma^2 eta^2/2 <= chi_m(eta) and \|chi_m(eta)\| <= C'_I rho'_I^{m-1} with the directed-rounding constants of the second-reader run (computations/verification/lineB/second_reader/logs/b1/b1_summary.json): rho'_I in [0.90000, 0.99982], C'_I <= 1.43e+07. The authors' sharper table (rho_I in [0.853, 0.99986], C_I <= 3.64e6; IEEE float with a-priori bound SAFE = 1 + 1e-9, libm exp/log <= 4 ulp) is a float certificate only and is not part of the proved statement. The upper bound is < 1 only for m >= m+(eta) (directed-rounding constants: about 1.2e+05 at eta = 1, 1e2-1e3 for most eta >= 15, at most 1.2e+05); it says nothing at m <= 13 nor, for eta <= 10, at the NSW-prime m of BGS-TRACE-168. eta in (81.6, 100] is not certified. Hypotheses of the directed-rounding proof: IEEE-754 binary64 round-to-nearest (outward rounding by nextafter; sparse mat-vec by the a-priori bound for nonnegative sums), python-flint/arb enclosures, and libm exp accurate to relative 1e-6 in the dropped-coefficient mass (a term <= 1e-29). |
| `BGS-TRACE-174` | **CONDITIONAL** | Decay of chi_m at every fixed eta != 0 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | Conditional on (I1)-(I3): for every eta != 0 the leading resonance of the parity transfer operator has modulus mu(eta) < 1 and \|chi_m(eta)\| <= C_eta (mu(eta) + eps)^m. Last step: a peripheral resonance would make the cocycle a continuous coboundary, which sums to zero on every periodic orbit, so X = 0 on the support, contradicting Prop L (BGS-TRACE-171). C_eta is not explicit; no rate is claimed. Citations in the packet's external_dependencies field, kept here because that field accepts only registered EXT ids: F. Faure, N. Roy, Nonlinearity 19 (2006) 1233-1252 (Thm 1, Prop 5; torus analogues); H. H. Rugh, Nonlinearity 5 (1992) 1237-1263 (analytic weights); J. von Neumann mean ergodic theorem (Walters, Thm 1.14; Reed-Simon I, Thm II.11); A. N. Livsic, Izv. Akad. Nauk SSSR Ser. Mat. 36 (1972) 1296-1320; M. Pollicott, C. Walkden, Trans. AMS 353 (2001) 2879-2895. |

Fixed eta; logarithmic window only (below Ehrenfest). |

B^k (1 - k^2 w_max/B) <= M_k <= k! B^k, so Z_m/sqrt(B_m) tends to a complex Gaussian in moments. Average over nu AND the Bohr variable eta, not at fixed eta. ND^(<=k) is not proved in general. |

| `BGS-TRACE-179` | **REFUTED** | Conjecture R: W~rest_m(eta) = o(m) uniformly on compact eta-ranges | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/derandomized_fixed_kick.md) | For 0 < eta0 <= eta1 < infinity, max_{eta in [eta0,eta1]} \|W~rest_m(eta)\| = o(m) as m -> infinity through odd m. (The NSW-prime version R_NSW, originally part of this entry, is BGS-TRACE-210.) Not reduced to a large-sieve inequality ('quadratic large sieve' phrasing withdrawn); Kurlberg-Rudnick, Bourgain and Bourgain-Glibichuk-Konyagin bounds do not apply since ord_h lambda = m is minimal. Derandomized analogue of the off-diagonal problem of BGS-TRACE-145 to 148; no progress on BGS-TRACE-148 is claimed. Line E annotation (2026-10-30, state unchanged): the k1-kick analogue of R is violated on a finite range (BGS-TRACE-187) and conditionally for all large m (BGS-TRACE-188); for this conjecture's kick \|W~rest_m(2)\| > m is certified at m = 11 and 13 only (BGS-TRACE-195, computer-assisted; an m = 17 certificate is pending and nothing is claimed for it) and asymptotic failure along 3 not dividing m holds only conditionally on H_infinity,7 (BGS-TRACE-196). R is not disproved. Line F/G annotation (2026-10-30, state unchanged): for the k1 kick the analogue of R now fails for every odd m >= 15 at eta = 2 unconditionally (computer-assisted, BGS-TRACE-199; also BGS-TRACE-201 at certified grid points), and for the pure 7-mode kick for 3 not dividing m (BGS-TRACE-200). For this conjecture's kick nothing changes: asymptotic failure is still only conditional on H_infinity,7 (BGS-TRACE-196). Conjecture R' (BGS-TRACE-206) is a separate, open conjecture about the k1 kick. R is not disproved. Line H annotation (2026-10-30): STATE CHANGED open -> refuted (Conjecture R as stated). For this conjecture's kick (Line B diagonal kick f_B, l = 0) at eta = 2, BGS-TRACE-209 (computer-assisted, on BGS-TRACE-207/208; independent referee audit, ACCEPT AFTER FIXES) gives W~rest_m(2)/m >= 1.4e4 for every odd m >= 47 with 3 not dividing m. Hence for every compact eta-range [eta0, eta1] containing 2, max_eta \|W~rest_m(eta)\| is not o(m), and R, which asserts o(m) for every compact range, is false. Scope of 'refuted': R exactly as stated here, for the Line B kick; it is not a statement about BGS (BGS-BGS-001 stays open; neither proved nor disproved) and not about BGS-TRACE-148. The NSW-prime version R_NSW, formerly part of this entry, is registered separately as BGS-TRACE-210 (open; split 2026-10-30). Not covered by BGS-TRACE-209: odd m with 3 not dividing m in {17,19,23,25,29,31,35,37,41,43} (m = 11, 13: BGS-TRACE-195), every m with 3 \| m, eta != 2 (in particular the restriction of R to compact ranges avoiding 2 is open and not registered separately), and s = +1. The statements 'R is not disproved' in the Line E and Line F/G annotations above are superseded by this annotation. m = 17 annotation (2026-10-31; state unchanged): for this conjecture's kick at eta = 2, an m = 17 certificate gives W~rest_17/m >= 42.9787 (author implementation only, not independently reproduced; see the BGS-TRACE-195 annotation and evidence/executions/20261031/m17/). It is evidence and not part of the refutation, and m = 17 is not added to the certified range of BGS-TRACE-195. |

| `BGS-TRACE-196` | **CONDITIONAL** | Line E E14: conditional asymptotic failure of Conjecture R for the Line B kick at eta = 2 along 3 not dividing m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/resonant_residue_lineE.md) | Hypothesis H_infinity,7, stated per sign s in {+1,-1}: the full-alphabet 49-state operators of BGS-TRACE-194 for the Line B kick at eta = 2 and that s are quasi-compact on a suitable Banach space with the trace formula, one sector has an isolated simple eigenvalue mu_s dominating all other spectrum of that s-family. If this holds for some s with \|mu_s\| > lambda^(1/4), then \|Z_m(nu_{7,s})\| ~ \|mu_s\|^m, F_m(2) >= C max_s (\|mu_s\|^2 lambda^(-1/2))^m over such s, and W~rest_m(2)/m -> infinity along odd m with 3 not dividing m: Conjecture R (BGS-TRACE-179) would fail at eta = 2. Nothing along 3 \| m. H_infinity,7 is not proved. Line F annotation (2026-10-30, state unchanged, stays conditional): the infinite-alphabet part of H_infinity,7 is reduced by the lift of BGS-TRACE-197 to a finite (about 24 000-dimensional per operator) ball-arithmetic certificate, which was not run. Float evidence (Line F F7, K = 14): leading \|mu\| = 1.8839 (s = +1) and 1.8968 (s = -1), next cluster 1.27-1.30 stable under two discretisations, lifted 49-state trace identity matching exact class sums to <= 5.9e-7. Line H annotation (2026-10-30, state unchanged): the CONCLUSION of this claim for s = -1 (\|Z_m(nu_{7,-1})\| ~ \|mu\|^m with \|mu\| > lambda^(1/4), W~rest_m(2)/m -> infinity along odd m with 3 not dividing m) is established unconditionally (computer-assisted) on the explicit lifted space of BGS-TRACE-197 by BGS-TRACE-207/208/209: \|mu*\| in [1.846834, 1.946835], W~rest_m(2)/m >= 1.4e4 for odd m >= 47, 3 not dividing m. s = +1 is not certified. The pre-registered m = 17 prediction remains untested by a certificate. m = 17 evidence annotation (2026-10-31; state unchanged, stays conditional): the pre-registered m = 17 predictions of the 49-state law (L = 48, K = 40; 5.66587e8 at N = -196 and 7.04767e8 at N = +196) were compared with the m = 17 certificate recorded in the BGS-TRACE-195 annotation (author implementation, not independently reproduced). The relative deviations are +7.2e-5 and +4.5e-5, the same order as at m = 11 and 13 (at most 6.3e-5 in absolute value). The predictions lie outside the certified enclosures, which have a relative width below 7e-8. No tolerance was pre-registered, so this is a consistency check of the 49-state law, not a pass/fail test. It is not part of the closure condition (H_infinity,7) and says nothing about m -> infinity. The sentence 'The pre-registered m = 17 prediction remains untested by a certificate' above is superseded (evidence/executions/20261031/m17/). |
| `BGS-TRACE-197` | **PROVED** | Line F F1: lifted vertex-minus-edge trace identity for the full-alphabet resonance transfer operators | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lifted_transfer_lineF.md) | Assume rho_V > lambda/(2(lambda-1)) ~ 0.6036, rho_E > 1/(2(lambda-1)) ~ 0.1036, beta > 0, and sum_n \|w(n)\| e^{alpha\|n\|} < infinity for some alpha > 2 pi c max(rho_V,rho_E) + beta/lambda (c = 1/(4 sqrt 2)). Then the vertex and edge operators L_V, L_E on weighted l2-direct sums of Hardy spaces over the disc lattices are trace class and, for every m >= 1, sum_{n in Z^m} prod_j w(n_j) e(c n_j s_j) = (1-lambda^-m)(Tr L_V^m - Tr L_E^m) (s_j the periodic orbit). With E1/E2 (BGS-TRACE-183/184): Z_m(nu*) = (1-lambda^-m) sum_{a in {0,2,4,6}} c_a (Tr L_{V,a}^m - Tr L_{E,a}^m), \|c_a\| = 1/2, for the FULL (untruncated) alphabet; real shifts (the 49-state law) are covered. Lean formalises only the counting step (#fixed points - #adjacent fixed pairs = 1, and adjacent fixed pairs = closed edge chains); trace class, the trace formula and the chain-to-block identification are paper arguments. Line H annotation (2026-10-30): the 49-state (real-shift x C^49) case is written out in BGS-TRACE-207. |

Reflection lemma (paper): (U Phi)_k(s) = Phi_{-k}(-s) (vertex), Phi_{-e-1}(-s) (edge) is unitary and U L_w U^-1 = L_{w(-.)}; for k1, L_{.,6} = U L_{.,2} U^-1. Referee re-certification with own code: \|mu*\| in [1.914473, 1.916474], other sectors below 1. Not a statement about the Line B kick. |

| `BGS-TRACE-203` | **PROVED** | Line G G1/G1'/G2: general (a,b) partial-trace transfer law for Z_m(nu), finite alphabet | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/resonance_classification_lineG.md) | For every unit nu and every (a,b) with b nu == a (mod 2h), b != 0, and every kick with finite support \|n\| <= L (rho > L/(lambda-1)): Z_m(nu) = (1-lambda^-m) Tr_H[(I x <1\|) L^m (I x \|delta_0>)] with L = sum_n ghat(n) R_n x A_n on H^2(D_rho) x C^{b^2}, R_n phi(s) = e(coef(n^2/2+ns)) phi((s+n)/lambda), coef = -4ac/b, A_n the automaton step for P mod b. P(n) = sum psi(\|i-j\|) n_i n_j exactly (BGS-TRACE-183). On each residue class of m mod the period of (k, y', h) mod b, Z_m(nu_{a,b}) is an exponential sum (1-lambda^-m) sum_mu c_mu mu^m over the eigenvalues of a fixed trace-class operator. Finite alphabet only: the infinite k1 alphabet would need a Line-F-type lift of the C^{b^2}-extended operator (not done). Galerkin truncations are numerical. |
| `BGS-TRACE-204` | **PROVED** | Line G G3: dilution identity along divisors d of h_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/resonance_classification_lineG.md) | For odd d \| h, t = j y' (mod d), gcd(t,d) = 1, G_d(t) = sum_{gamma in O/d} e(-t Nm(gamma)/d), \|G_d(t)\| = d: Z(nu0 + 2hj/d) = (G_d(t)/d^2) sum_{beta in O/d} e(tbar Nm(beta)/d) Z^(beta)_{nu0} (O = Z[sqrt 2]; Z^(beta) = sum_n w(n) e(Tr(beta alpha_n)/d), i.e. the kick at site i translated by Tr(beta lambda^i)/d; the sum is invariant under beta -> -beta, so the sign of the translation is immaterial). Proof (31 October 2026, owner audit of PR #60): Prop I2' (BGS-TRACE-212) gives Z(nu0 + 2hj/d) = d^-2 sum_beta Zhat(beta) G_t(beta) with G_t(beta) = sum_gamma e(-(t Nm gamma + Tr beta gamma)/d) and Zhat = Z^(beta); completing the square, t Nm(gamma) + Tr(beta gamma) = t Nm(gamma + tbar conj(beta)) - tbar Nm(beta) in Z/d, and gamma -> gamma + tbar conj(beta) is a bijection of O/d, so G_t(beta) = e(tbar Nm(beta)/d) G_d(t), which is the displayed formula, for every odd d \| h (prime or composite) and every j with t a unit (written derivation: mathematics/fixed-map/lineI_rprime_reduction.md section 3). Checks: Line G brute force for prime d (7 at m = 9, 41 at m = 5; 2.8e-14); Line I brute force of the displayed formula and of the completed square for every beta, random site-dependent weights, d = 41 (m = 5), 239 (7), 7, 199, 1393 (9), 23, 353 (11), 79, 599 (13), 7, 31, 41, 217, 287, 961, 1271 (15), 7, 49, 1673 (21), <= 8.4e-15 (negative control with tbar -> -tbar, run and logged by lineI_G3_form_check.py, fails in every case with error >= 1.19). Not claimed: the main-term approximation \|Z\|^2 ~ \|Z_rho\|^2/d^2 has no rigorous error bound (observed 0.1-37%); the size of the beta != 0 terms is not bounded. |

| `BGS-TRACE-207` | **PROVED** | Line H: kappa lemma (\|c_b\| = 1/2 for all odd m, 3 not dividing m) and the full-alphabet 49-state lifted trace identity for the Line B kick | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineH_49state_certificate.md) | For every odd m with 3 not dividing m and s = +-1: kappa = -k u y' mod 8 is odd (h^2+1 = 2y'^2 forces h, y' odd; 196 nu + s = k h forces k odd; u = 49^-1 == 1 mod 8), hence c_b = 8^-1 sum_t e((kappa t^2 - b t)/8) has \|c_b\| = 1/2 (b even), 0 (b odd). Proof: block trace factorises as Tr(M_p C_psi) Tr(U_{n_{m-1}}...U_{n_0}); the C^49 factor is a monomial matrix whose permutation sigma -> lambda_7^-m sigma + const has exactly one fixed point iff 3 does not divide m, giving the TRACE-194 phase; vertex-minus-edge counting (Bgs.LineF.vertex_minus_edge, real shifts); absolute trace-norm convergence. | `BGS-TRACE-212` | **PROVED** | Line I Prop I2/I2': dilation-coset L^2 law and per-member Gauss-sum formula for the members Z_m(nu0 + 2hj/d), d odd, d \| h_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineI_rprime_reduction.md) | Odd m, h = h_m, y' with h^2+1 = 2y'^2, alpha_n = sum_i n_i lambda^i in Z[sqrt2], any unit nu0, w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), Z0 = Z_m(nu0). Congruence: P(n) == y' Nm(alpha_n) (mod 2h). (I2') For every odd d \| h (prime or composite) and j with t = j y' a unit mod d: Z_m(nu0 + 2hj/d) = sum_gamma W(gamma) e(-t Nm(gamma)/d) = d^-2 sum_{beta mod d} Zhat(beta) G_t(beta), W(gamma) = sum_{alpha_n == gamma mod d} w(n), Zhat(beta) = sum_n w(n) e(Tr(beta alpha_n)/d) (twisted sums: kick at site i translated by Tr(beta lambda^i)/d), G_t(beta) = sum_gamma e(-(t Nm gamma + Tr beta gamma)/d), \|G_t(beta)\| = d; hence Z_t = omega_t Z0/d + Delta_t, \|omega_t\| = 1, \|Delta_t\| <= d^-1 sum_{beta != 0} \|Zhat(beta)\|. (I2) For prime d \|\| h, eps = (2/d), W_u = sum_{Nm gamma == u} W(gamma), Z_t = sum_u W_u e(-tu/d): (a) sum_t \|Z_t\|^2 = d sum_u \|W_u\|^2; (b) N(u) = d - eps (u != 0), N(0) = d + (d-1) eps; (c) with delta_u = W_u - Z0 N(u)/d^2: sum_u N(u) delta_u = eps d delta_0, sum_u N(u)^2 = d^3 + d^2 - d; (d) sum_{t != 0} \|Z_t\|^2 = \|Z0\|^2 (1/d - 1/d^2) + 2 eps Re(conj(Z0) delta_0) + d sum_u \|delta_u\|^2. For odd m, eps = +1 for every prime d \| h_m (Lean two_isSq_mod). Lean covers the algebra (c)-(d) (Bgs.LineI: cross_term, sum_N_sq, coset_decomp), the norm counts over Z/7 and the zero count over Z/23, eps = +1 and 7 \| h_m <=> m == 3 mod 6 (Bgs.LineIArith); the Fourier step, the congruence and I2' are paper proofs. Completing the square, G_t(beta) = e(tbar Nm(beta)/d) G_d(t), turns I2' into the Line G G3 form (BGS-TRACE-204, now proved from this claim; derivation in the source note, section 3). Does not bound the twisted sums. |
| `BGS-TRACE-213` | **PROVED** | Line I Thm I4: exact reduction of Conjecture R' to (E''), (T'') and the dilation remainder (D) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineI_rprime_reduction.md) | Frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (k1, eta = 2, odd m >= 5, any set of included levels): the sets {nu_rho} and M_d(rho) are pairwise disjoint and W'_m = phi^-1 sum_{nu unit, nu not resonant} (\|Z_nu\|^2 - RMT_m) + phi^-1 sum_{rho, d included} Dev_d(rho), Dev_d(rho) = sum_{nu in M_d(rho)} (\|Z_nu\|^2 - \|Z_rho\|^2/d^2) (exact identity). Splitting the non-resonant units by the height y of the reduced representative of z_nu = (nu+i)/2h at ONE function Y(m) -> infinity: if (E'') the part with y <= Y(m) (uniformly on the growing compacta K_m, not merely each fixed compact), (T'') the part with y > Y(m) and (D) the dilation remainder phi^-1 sum Dev are each o(m), then R' (W'_m = o(m)) holds. Alternative sufficient (not equivalent) hypothesis: (T'') for every Y(m) -> infinity and (E'') for each fixed Y, with a diagonal choice of Y(m). Sums over units, normalised by 1/phi(2h). None of (E''), (T''), (D) is proved; for d = 7 only the square part of (D) is controlled, for rho = (+-2,8) only; the (+-2,16) level-7 members are not controlled (BGS-TRACE-215), and the operator-radius route to the cross part fails (BGS-TRACE-217). A reduction, not progress on the truth of R'. |

The diagonal class P(n) == P(n') contributes C(0) times the UNWEIGHTED sum of f over the 2h (resp. phi(2h) primitive) horocycle points and meets every c_{2hj}; it is not invisible for cusp forms (the earlier 'diagonal invisible' statement is withdrawn and not registered). Using (b) for R' would need a horocycle equidistribution input with an explicit rate and C(0) = O(m); neither is part of this claim. |

| `BGS-TRACE-228` | **PROVED** | Line J3 inheritance theorem: W_{ce, e beta'} = (n_e/e^2) W_{c, beta'} for coprime odd c, e with ce \| h_m (composite levels carry positive multiples of lower-level class sums) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineJ_d7_twisted_coefficient.md) | Odd m, h = h_m, frozen member sets M_d(rho) of BGS-TRACE-206, O = Z[sqrt2]. Let c, e be coprime odd integers with c > 1, ce \| h_m, d = ce, beta' in O/c, beta = e beta'. Then (i) Zhat^(d)_beta = Zhat^(c)_beta' and (ii) W_{d,beta} = (n_e/e^2) W_{c,beta'}, where n_e = #{j mod e : j a unit mod e and nu0 + (2h/d) j not == 0 mod l for every prime l \| e with v_l(h) = v_l(d)} >= 0, independent of c, beta' and the sign of rho; for a prime q, n_q = q - 2 if q \|\| h and q - 1 if q^2 \| h. Proof by CRT (x/d == x ebar/c + x cbar/e mod 1), \|G^(e)_u(0)\|^2 = e^2 for units u, and independence of the mod-e conditions. Consequences: every composite-level copy of the d = 7 cross term is a positive multiple of it, summing to A_m = 1 + sum n_D'/D'^2 in [1, sigma(h)/h], the same for +-rho (so the 1:3 cancellation of BGS-TRACE-221 persists); square parts of every Dev_d are >= 0. Lean (Bgs.LineJArith: count_excl_one, count_units) checks only the finite counts q - 2 and q - 1; the CRT/Gauss-sum argument is a paper proof. Exact check: class sums W_C compared in arb from exact integer histograms to <= 9e-56 at (m, a, q) = (15, 2, 41), (15, -2, 31), (33, 2, 23). |
| `BGS-TRACE-229` | **PROVED** | Line J3: prime-level complete-sum bound: \|W_{q,beta}\| <= 2/q^2 for non-isotropic beta, (q-2)/q^2 for the 2(q-1) isotropic beta (q \|\| h_m) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineJ_d7_twisted_coefficient.md) | Odd m, h = h_m, prime q \|\| h_m, frozen members of level q. Completing the square, conj G_t(0) G_t(beta) = q^2 e(N(beta) tbar/q); the labels t = j y' cover F_q^* except exactly one value t_b. Hence W_{q,beta} = q^-2 (-1 - e(N(beta) tbar_b/q)), \|W_{q,beta}\| <= 2/q^2, for non-isotropic beta (N(beta) != 0 mod q), and W_{q,beta} = (q-2)/q^2 for the 2(q-1) isotropic beta != 0 (2 is a square mod q as 2y'^2 == 1). If q^2 \| h there is no missing label and the values are 1/q^2 and (q-1)/q^2. A bound on the complete Gauss-sum weights only; it does not bound the twisted sums Zhat(beta). That this suppresses the slow-twist danger on m = 3p is a heuristic, not part of the claim. Float check: q = 41 max q^2\|W\| = 1.994 (non-isotropic), 39 (isotropic); q = 31: 1.000, 30. |
| `BGS-TRACE-230` | **PROVED** | Line J3 r(q) lemma: for odd m and an odd prime q, q \| h_m iff r(q) := ord_q(lambda) divides m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineJ_d7_twisted_coefficient.md) | h_m = (epsilon^m + epsilonbar^m)/2 with epsilon = 1 + sqrt2, epsilonbar = -epsilon^-1, lambda = epsilon^2 = 3 + 2 sqrt2. For an odd prime q let r(q) be the multiplicative order of lambda in (Z[sqrt2]/q)^*. For odd m: 2 h_m = epsilon^m - epsilon^-m, so q \| h_m iff epsilon^(2m) == 1 iff lambda^m == 1 iff r(q) \| m (h is the companion V-type sequence, so this is not the standard U-type rank rule). Corollary: if r(q) = p is an odd prime then q == 1 (mod 2p); on m = 3p (p prime, p != 7) every prime q != 7 dividing h_m has rank p or 3p, and 49 does not divide h_m (lambda has order 21 mod 49). Lean (Bgs.LineJArith: rank_core) proves the ring identity: for a unit epsilon of any commutative ring and odd m, epsilon^m + (-epsilon^-1)^m = 0 iff (epsilon^2)^m = 1; pell49_lambda_order proves the order 21 of lambda mod 49 (coordinates). The passage to Z[sqrt2]/q and the corollary are paper steps. That small-rank levels dominate elsewhere (so m = 3p is the natural subsequence) is numerical/heuristic and not claimed. |

O-natural_m = sum of the cross parts over (rho in P*, included d, beta of conductor not in {1, 7}) (prime(-power) levels of rank p or 3p, their inherited copies, mixed 7c parts). (1) [P] Identity (BGS-TRACE-212, 213, 228): W'_m = W'_A + phi^-1 [A_m X7_m + X16_m + Q_m + O-natural_m], X7 = d = 7 cross part of (+-2,8), X16 = d = 7 part of (+-2,16) with inherited copies, Q_m >= 0, A_m >= 1. (2) [CA] On S+: X7_m >= (K_j/2) xi_lo^m (BGS-TRACE-225), \|X16_m\| <= 0.01 (K_min/2) xi_lo^m (BGS-TRACE-227), W'_A >= -4m; hence W'_m >= -4m + 0.99 K_j 1.0184224^m + phi^-1 O-natural_m on S+. (3) [P, external theorems] Under H_arg (BGS-TRACE-233), S+ is infinite, with relative density arccos(0.84431)/pi = 0.18001 among primes p == p0 (mod 56), p >= 167 (Vinogradov's theorem on sum_{p<=x} e(p gamma) = o(pi(x)) for irrational gamma, EXT-VINOGRADOV-1937; prime number theorem in arithmetic progressions, EXT-DAVENPORT-1980; Weyl criterion, EXT-WEYL-1916). [CA] arg xi/2 pi in [0.88797591, 0.88797627] is not a rational with denominator < 1339 (rat_excl.py, outward rounded). Corollary (referee wording): frozen R' (BGS-TRACE-206) is false along m = 3p under H-natural (BGS-TRACE-232) and H_arg (BGS-TRACE-233); unconditionally, if R' holds, the conductor-not-in-{1,7} parts must cancel the d = 7 term exponentially on S+: R' implies phi^-1 O-natural_m <= -0.99 K_j 1.0184^m + 4m + o(m) along S+ (S+ infinite under H_arg). A conditional implication: H-natural and H_arg are open, and BGS-TRACE-206 is NOT refuted. Line K annotation (2026-10-31; state unchanged, conditional): S+ is infinite unconditionally by Theorem K3 (BGS-TRACE-235; H_arg removed by a case split, not proved), so the conclusion of this claim holds under H-natural (BGS-TRACE-232) alone, and the dichotomy holds on an infinite S+ unconditionally (Corollary K3, BGS-TRACE-236). The dependency on H_arg (BGS-TRACE-233) is replaced by BGS-TRACE-235. BGS-TRACE-206 is NOT refuted. |

| `BGS-TRACE-234` | **PROVED** | Line K Lemma K3 (gap lemma): for b' >= 447, b' not in {504, 560, 616, 840}, every unit class mod gcd(56, b') has maximal cyclic gap < A b' among the units mod b' in that class (A = arccos(0.84431)/pi) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineK_harg_removal.md) | A = arccos(0.84431)/pi, arb lower bound 0.18001064843. For b' >= 447 with b' not in {504, 560, 616, 840} and every unit class r mod g' = gcd(56, b'), V(b', r) = {v mod b' : gcd(v, b') = 1, v == r (mod g')} has maximal cyclic gap < A b'. (a) [CA, exact integers] 447 <= b' <= 6000: only 504, 560, 616, 840 fail; every other b' has gap <= b'/6 (smallest margin A - 1/6 = 0.01334 at b' = 672) (k3_harg.py; referee exact-rational ref_k3.py; integration check lineK_checks.py on 447..2645). (b) [P] sieve: v = r + g't is admissible iff gcd(r + g't, R) = 1 (R = rad of the part of b' coprime to g'; 2, 7 divide g'), inclusion-exclusion gives gap in t <= floor(2^k R/phi(R)) + 1 <= F + 1, F = prod 2l/(l-1); a failure needs prod_{l\|R} 2/(l-1) >= A - 1/R, impossible for any prime l >= 23; the subsets of {3,5,11,13,17,19} leave exactly 9 survivors, each with b' <= 56(F+1)/A <= 2644.3, inside the exact range. Lean (Bgs.LineKFinite): sieve enumeration and survivors, the l = 23 inequality, 1/6 < 18001/100000, and gap <= b'/6 for 447 <= b' <= 466 in lake build Bgs; the full range 447..2645 by the author's kernel-decide chunk files (computations/verification/lineK/lean/) outside lake build Bgs: 61 recompiled at integration as written; the 100-modulus chunk 1547..1646 exceeded the memory of the 15 GB box and was re-checked as five 20-modulus chunks with identical definitions (evidence/executions/20261031/lineK/lean_gaps/split_1547/); all exit 0 and axiom-free. Not formalised: the inclusion-exclusion inequality, the monotonicity step for l > 23, the arb bound 0.18001 <= A. |
| `BGS-TRACE-235` | **PROVED** | Line K Theorem K3: S+ contains infinitely many m = 3p with p == p0 (mod 56) for every unit p0 mod 56, unconditionally (H_arg removed) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineK_harg_removal.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 (S+, O-natural_m, K_j, xi, xi_lo = 2.4586893). Proof by a case split on an undecided fact (it does not say which case holds). Irrational arg xi/2 pi: equidistribution over p == p0 (mod 56) (EXT-VINOGRADOV-1937, EXT-DAVENPORT-1980, EXT-WEYL-1916; the Line J3 argument, density 0.18001). Rational a/b in lowest terms: b >= 1339 (J3 rat_excl.py, certified outward-rounded enclosure [0.88797591, 0.88797627] of arg xi/2 pi; Lean no_small_denominator); b' = b/gcd(3,b) >= 447, a' = 3a/gcd(3,b), gcd(a', b') = 1; p == p0 (56) and p == v (b') are compatible iff v == p0 (mod g'), the joint class is a unit iff gcd(v, b') = 1, so by Dirichlet (EXT-DIRICHLET-1837) every v in V(b', p0 mod g') is hit by infinitely many primes p == p0 (56); a' V(b', r) = V(b', a'r) as sets, so by Lemma K3 (BGS-TRACE-234, every unit class) the phases a'v/b' meet every closed arc of length A, in particular the arc cos(arg B_j + 2 pi x) >= 0.84431; the exceptional b' in {504, 560, 616, 840} give b in {1512, 560, 1680, 616, 1848, 2520}, none with a fraction in the enclosure (exact; Lean exceptions_excluded); B_j is constant on the class (j = 3p mod 168) and m >= M0(j) for large p. No density is claimed in the rational case. H_arg (BGS-TRACE-233) is bypassed, not proved. |

| `BGS-TRACE-237` | **PROVED** | Line K Theorem K1 (light-cone identity): for a prime q != 7, q \|\| h_m, 2 a square mod q, sum_{beta != 0} Zhat_beta W_{q,beta} = D_iso - E_b/q | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineK_harg_removal.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 (S+, O-natural_m, K_j, xi, xi_lo = 2.4586893). q != 7 prime, q \|\| h_m, and 2 a square mod q (q == +-1 mod 8; automatic for q \| h_m since 2 y'^2 == 1 mod q), so qO = q qbar splits. Grouping words by x_w = sum_i n_i lambda^i in O/q (legitimate by absolute convergence, L16 step 3 / Line F) gives Zhat_beta = sum_x A(x) e(Tr(beta x)/q), A_tot = Zhat_0. Then sum_{beta != 0} Zhat_beta W_{q,beta} = D_iso - E_b/q with D_iso = A(q u qbar) - ((2q-1)/q^2) A_tot, E_b = sum_x A(x) e(-N(x) t_b/q) - A_tot/q, t_b the missing label (proof: BGS-TRACE-229 form of W, completing the square, Gauss sum G(u)G(-2u) = (2/q) q = q, beta = 0 term (q-2) A_tot/q^2, #{N(x) == 0} = 2q - 1). Exact reformulation [P]: D_iso and E_b depend on A only through its push-forward under x -> N(x) mod q, so the level-q cross part vanishes iff D_iso = E_b/q. Checks: author 40-digit random A, q = 7, 17, 23, 31 (<= 2e-39); referee exact in Z[zeta_q] for every delta-mass and every t_b, q = 7, 17, 23, 31, 41, 47 (0 mismatches in 214,240 cases); control q = 11, 13 (2 non-square) fails in every case. NOT registered (interpretation [H]): 'trivial zeros are exactly the words with x_w in the intersection of the q', 'sporadic zeros are unavoidable for q <~ C^p', 'H-natural at prime levels iff equidistribution on q u qbar' (E_b is not negligible a priori). |

| `BGS-TRACE-241` | **PROVED** | Line K Lemma W1 (Weil bound over the orbit subgroup): for q prime, H = <l> subset F_q^* of order r and A, B in F_q not both 0, \|sum_{h in H} e((Ah + B/h)/q)\| <= 2 sqrt q (<= sqrt q if AB = 0); degeneracy criterion | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineK_harg_removal.md) | Scope: m = 3p, q != 7 prime, q \| h_m, r = r(q) = ord_q(lambda) in {p, 3p} (BGS-TRACE-230), q == +-1 mod 8, sigma, sigmabar: O/q -> F_q, l = sigma(lambda), sigmabar(lambda) = l^-1, H = <l> of order r. [P-cite] For A, B in F_q not both 0: \|sum_{h in H} e((Ah + B h^-1)/q)\| <= 2 sqrt q, and <= sqrt q if AB = 0 (characters of F_q^* trivial on H detect H; each inner sum is a twisted Kloosterman sum bounded by 2 sqrt q (Weil, EXT-WEIL-1948) or, for AB = 0, a Gauss sum of modulus sqrt q (chi != 1) or -1 (chi = 1), giving ((e-1) sqrt q + 1)/e <= sqrt q, e = (q-1)/r). Application: for beta != 0 and gamma in O, sum_{i<r} e(Tr(gamma beta lambda^i)/q) is this sum with (A, B) = (sigma(gamma beta), sigmabar(gamma beta)); it is degenerate iff gamma beta == 0 mod q: for a unit beta iff gamma in qO (\|N(gamma)\| >= q^2), for isotropic beta in qbar minus 0 iff gamma in q (\|N(gamma)\| >= q). Conductor 7q: split i mod 3 (lambda has order 3 mod 7), <= 6 sqrt q with the same degeneracy condition at q. [N] instance check w1_weil_check.py (20 pairs (p, q), exhaustive over A, B; max ratio 1.19 binomial, 0.59 linear). |

| `BGS-TRACE-246` | **PROVED** | Line K Theorem K2' (OP proved in Line L; under the standing inputs): for m in S+ with m >= m1 (effective, not computed), frozen R' implies that some open conductor c of h_m carries a slow lambda-orbit | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineK_harg_removal.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 (S+, O-natural_m, K_j, xi, xi_lo = 2.4586893). For m = 3p in S+, p >= p0, a conductor c \| h_m is open if c not in {1, 7} u {q, 7q : q <= c_k p^2} and c < L_m (vacuous for m <~ 1.68e9 [N]); beta at an open conductor c is slow if (1/r) sum_{i<r} f_k^F(beta lambda^i/c) > ((1 - delta/2) target_F)^{2k} for some family F. [P] given (OP): if no open conductor of h_m has a slow beta, the open part of O-natural_m is o(xi_lo^m) (W2'), the Weil part is o(xi_lo^m) (Theorem W), so O-natural^{<L} = o(xi_lo^m), H-natural_mid holds along such m and W'_m/m -> infinity (Theorem K, BGS-TRACE-240). Hence frozen R' implies: for all large m in S+ (an infinite set, BGS-TRACE-235) some open conductor carries a slow lambda-orbit. For given m, c, beta this is checkable in principle by a certified operator computation (truncation with rigorous error). Conductors c >= L_m occur only at absorbed levels; prime powers and composites are included in 'open'. Line K status (superseded below): conditional on (OP). BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. Lines L, L2, L3 (2026-10-31; BGS-TRACE-248..268, mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md): explicit state review conditional -> proved [P + CA], under the same standing inputs as BGS-TRACE-245 (Theorem J (BGS-TRACE-225), Lemma L16 (BGS-TRACE-226), the (+-2,16) level-7 control (BGS-TRACE-227) and the proved parts (1) identity (BGS-TRACE-212, 213, 228) and (2) [CA] bounds (BGS-TRACE-225, 227) of Theorem J3 (BGS-TRACE-231, whose conditional H-natural conclusion is not used), S_Z / Prop K2 BGS-TRACE-238, Weil bound W1 BGS-TRACE-241; all proved). Theorem K (BGS-TRACE-240, conditional) is NOT a dependency: only its proved implication is used, re-derived from its proved components (BGS-TRACE-235 (1), the proved parts of BGS-TRACE-231 with BGS-TRACE-238 (2), BGS-TRACE-237 (3)): if O-natural^{<L}_m = o(xi_lo^m) along an infinite subset of S+ then W'_m/m -> infinity (frozen R' false); neither H-natural nor H-natural_mid is assumed. Statement: for all sufficiently large m = 3p in S+ (m >= m1, effective, not computed), frozen R' implies a slow lambda-orbit at an open conductor c \| h_m, c not in {1, 7} u {q, 7q : q <= c_W p^2}, c < L_m (for (+-2,16): c < (1.6677^2/xi_lo)^{m(1+eta)}, BGS-TRACE-253), c_W = 10^-25.62, q0 = 10^13.2; slow means (1/r) sum_{i<r} f_k^F(beta lambda^i/c) > ((1 - delta_F/2) target_F)^{2k} for some family F, with the k_F, delta_F of BGS-TRACE-252. No explicit p0 is claimed. CAVEAT (Prop S, BGS-TRACE-258): K2' is true, but it is NOT a refutation route for the m covered by Prop S (m in S+ such that h_m has a prime q \|\| h_m in (max(c_W p^2, lambda^{C_k + 6.1e-6 m}), L_m)): there the conclusion of K2' holds without assuming frozen R', so it yields no contradiction. That this covers all or most m (an earlier [H] caveat) is superseded by Theorem N3 [P] (BGS-TRACE-283, on the certified bound of BGS-TRACE-198): for every p >= p0 (effective, not computed) the conductor c = h_p, which lies in K2''s open set (open for the (+-2,8) families; slow orbit in (+-2,8)V; for (+-2,16) h_p is absorbed, BGS-TRACE-253) (M3, BGS-TRACE-271), carries the slow orbit beta = 1, so K2''s conclusion holds without assuming frozen R' and K2' is never a refutation route for such m (Corollary N3', BGS-TRACE-284). The dependency on BGS-TRACE-283 is annotational only: K2' itself does not use N3 (owner audit of PR #65, S3). The open-range target is the mean statement H_mean (BGS-TRACE-256, open; H_mean implies the refutation, BGS-TRACE-257). BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. |

| `BGS-TRACE-248` | **PROVED** | Line L collision structure: alpha_v = sum_j v_j lambda^j = 0 iff (X^2 - 6X + 1) \| v(X); carries and the Green's-function bound | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | lambda = 3 + 2 sqrt2 has minimal polynomial X^2 - 6X + 1, so for v in Z^k, alpha_v = 0 iff v(X) = (X^2 - 6X + 1) E(X) with E in Z[X]. The coefficients e_j of E are carries, v_j = e_j - 6 e_{j-1} + e_{j-2}, and \|e_j\| <= C0g sum_i \|v_i\| lambda^{-\|j+1-i\|} with C0g = 1/(lambda - lambda^-1) (Green's function of the recursion). Hence the exact-collision sums S_k of BGS-TRACE-250 are transfer-matrix sums over carry states (e_{j-1}, e_j). Referee check: the only collision with \|v_j\| <= 3 at k = 6 is v = 0. |
