[P] \|N(alpha_v)\| <= XY <= ((aX + Y/a)/2)^2, X = sum \|v_j\| lambda^j, Y = sum \|v_j\| lambda^-j, a = lambda^{-(k-1)/2}; hence T_k(q) <= C_pair e^{-2 s sqrt q} prod_j A(tau_j + s(a lambda^j + lambda^-j/a))^2 for every s > 0 (Chernoff), monotone in q. [CA] evaluated in arb on an s-grid and a log10 q grid of step 0.1. Per family F: delta_F = (1 - rate_F/target_F)/2 = delta_OP/2 (delta_OP = 1 - rate_F/target_F as in BGS-TRACE-244), eta_F = (((1 - delta_F/2) target_F)^{2k} - I_k)/3, c_F = (eta_F/(6 A_k))^2, q0_F with T_k(q0_F) <= eta_F: (+-2,8)V delta 0.00811, log10 c_F -25.62, log10 q0 13.2; (+-2,8)E 0.0573, -20.93, 12.3; (+-2,16)V 0.0277, -22.53, 12.3; (+-2,16)E 0.0581, -20.84, 12.2. c_W := 10^-25.62 <= min_F c_F and q0 := 10^13.2 = max_F q0_F. Non-vacuity c_W p^2 >= 2p + 1 needs p >= 2/c_W = 8.34e25 (the report rounds to 8.2e25). These constants are explicit; the threshold m1 of Theorem W (BGS-TRACE-245) is effective but NOT computed; no explicit p0 is claimed. |
| `BGS-TRACE-253` | **PROVED** | Line L (+-2,16) narrowing: the (+-2,16) levels are absorbed for q >= (1.6677^2/xi_lo)^{m(1+eta)}, eta <= (4.282 + o(1))/log(2p+1), so the (+-2,16) open range is c_W p^2 < q < (1.6677^2/xi_lo)^{m(1+eta)} (1.6677^2/xi_lo = 1.13118...) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Standing inputs: the Line F/J lattice trace representation and its radius hypotheses (BGS-TRACE-197), Theorem J (BGS-TRACE-225), Lemma L16 (BGS-TRACE-226), the (+-2,16) level-7 control (BGS-TRACE-227) and the proved parts (1) identity (BGS-TRACE-212, 213, 228) and (2) [CA] bounds (BGS-TRACE-225, 227) of Theorem J3 (BGS-TRACE-231, whose conditional H-natural conclusion is not used), S_Z / Prop K2 (BGS-TRACE-238) and the Weil bound W1 (BGS-TRACE-241). [P + CA] With d <= \|Z\|/(2 sqrt m) and positivity absorption (Prop K2, BGS-TRACE-238, with Lambda_16 = log(1.6677^2/xi_lo) in place of Lambda and the (+-2,16) constant C <= 3.8e-25 in place of S_Z), the (+-2,16) levels are absorbed for q >= (1.6677^2/xi_lo)^{m(1+eta)}, eta = eta_m^{(16)} <= (0.4043/Lambda_16 + 1)/log(2p+1) + O(log l/m) = (4.282 + o(1))/log(2p+1), l = log(2p+1) (referee F5: eta specified). Integration note: 1.6677^2/xi_lo = 1.13118 and Lambda_16 = 0.12326; REPORT_L_v2 and the referee quote 1.1311 and 0.12319 (rounded down, which slightly understates the open range); registered here in the exact form; 0.4043/0.12326 + 1 = 4.27999 <= 4.282 (lineL_checks.py). For (+-2,8) there is no narrowing (L_m = 1.4923^{m(1+eta_m)}). |
| `BGS-TRACE-254` | **PROVED** | Line L Theorem U (level-h scalar L^2 unitarity): the TRACE-206 autocorrelation R(v) vanishes at every exact collision and every collision mod h, so sum_x \|A(x)\|^2 = 1 and sum_{beta mod h} \|Zhat(beta/h)\|^2 = h^2 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), Phihat(n) = prod_j ghat(n_j), alpha_n = sum_j n_j lambda^j, A(x) = sum_{alpha_n = x} w(n), Zhat(beta) = sum_n w(n) e(Tr(beta alpha_n)/q). Lemma U1 [P]: R(v) := sum_n w(n) conj w(n - v) = e(nu0 P(v)/2h) prod_j kappa(v_j, theta_j), kappa(d, theta) = sum_a ghat(a) conj ghat(a - d) e(-a theta), theta_j = nu0 y' Tr(lambda^-j alpha_v)/(2h) (P quadratic, cross term linear, psi(d) = y' x_d - 2h y_d, x_d = Tr(lambda^d)/2). Theorem U [P]: if alpha_v = 0 or alpha_v in hO then all theta_j in Z and kappa(d, 0) = i^d J_d(0) = delta_{d,0} (Neumann/Graf addition theorem), so R(v) = delta_{v,0} sum \|w\|^2; hence sum_{x in O} \|A(x)\|^2 = 1 and sum_{beta mod h} \|Zhat(beta/h)\|^2 = h^2. At a level q \| h, (1/q^2) sum_beta \|Zhat_beta\|^2 = sum_{alpha_v in qO} R(v) is NOT 1 in general (twist at level h/q). Link to Conjecture C1 (BGS-TRACE-247): Theorem U is the exact L^2 version behind C1 and does NOT imply C1; it controls no individual level. Referee caveat: the congruence form of the TRACE-206 phase P(n) (the psi_d coefficients) is taken as given, not re-derived (supported numerically only). [N] level-h Parseval at m = 5 (h = 41, \|n_j\| <= 4) to 3e-11 (l1_unit_m5_L4.log); referee extended to m = 7, 9. |
| `BGS-TRACE-255` | **PROVED** | Line L2 decomposition: D_q = (1/q) sum_{beta in l(q) minus 0} Zhat_beta, D_iso = D_q + D_qbar - (1/q^2) sum_{beta != 0} Zhat_beta (open prime levels q \|\| h_m) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). q \|\| h_m an open prime level, qO = q qbar split; l(a) subset O/q the annihilator of an ideal a containing qO under (beta, x) -> e(Tr(beta x)/q), l(q), l(qbar) the two isotropic lines. [P] A(a) = [O:a]^-1 sum_{beta in l(a)} Zhat_beta (orthogonality); D_q := A(q) - A_tot/q = (1/q) sum_{beta in l(q) minus 0} Zhat_beta; D_iso = D_q + D_qbar - (A(qO) - A_tot/q^2) (q cap qbar = qO, (2q-1)/q^2 = 2/q - 1/q^2); A(qO) - A_tot/q^2 = (1/q^2) sum_{beta != 0} Zhat_beta. With Theorem K1 (BGS-TRACE-237) the level contributes 2 Re(conj Z_rho (D_iso - E_b/q)): averages over beta with weights 1/q (isotropic lines) and about 1/q^2 elsewhere, not per-beta bounds. |
| `BGS-TRACE-256` | **OPEN** | Line L2 hypothesis H_mean: for some delta > 0 and infinitely many m in S+, \|D_iso(q) - E_b(q)/q\| <= ((1 - delta) target)^m at every open prime level q, with the analogue at open composite levels | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | A proof (or disproof) that for some delta > 0 and infinitely many m in S+ the K1 combination D_iso - E_b/q is <= ((1 - delta) target)^m at every open prime level (and the analogue at open composite levels); a proof would refute frozen R' (BGS-TRACE-257). |
| `BGS-TRACE-257` | **PROVED** | Line L2: H_mean implies that frozen R' is false along m = 3p (a proved implication; H_mean is open) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Standing inputs: the Line F/J lattice trace representation and its radius hypotheses (BGS-TRACE-197), Theorem J (BGS-TRACE-225), Lemma L16 (BGS-TRACE-226), the (+-2,16) level-7 control (BGS-TRACE-227) and the proved parts (1) identity (BGS-TRACE-212, 213, 228) and (2) [CA] bounds (BGS-TRACE-225, 227) of Theorem J3 (BGS-TRACE-231, whose conditional H-natural conclusion is not used), S_Z / Prop K2 (BGS-TRACE-238) and the Weil bound W1 (BGS-TRACE-241). [P] If H_mean (BGS-TRACE-256) holds then, by Theorem K1 (BGS-TRACE-237: each open prime level contributes 2 Re(conj Z_rho (D_iso - E_b/q)), \|Z_rho\| <= C_rho mu^m with mu * 1.28359 < xi_lo and 1.6677 * 1.4742 < xi_lo), Prop K2 (BGS-TRACE-238; levels >= L_m absorbed) and Theorem W (BGS-TRACE-245; Weil range negligible, m >= m1 effective, not computed), the open part of O-natural is o(xi_lo^m) up to e^{o(m)} level counts, so H-natural_mid holds along these m and the proved implication of Theorem K gives W'_m/m -> infinity: frozen R' is false. Theorem K (BGS-TRACE-240, conditional) is NOT a dependency: only its proved implication is used, re-derived from its proved components (BGS-TRACE-235 (1), the proved parts of BGS-TRACE-231 with BGS-TRACE-238 (2), BGS-TRACE-237 (3)): if O-natural^{<L}_m = o(xi_lo^m) along an infinite subset of S+ then W'_m/m -> infinity (frozen R' false); neither H-natural nor H-natural_mid is assumed. H_mean (BGS-TRACE-256, open) is the antecedent of this implication and is therefore NOT listed as a dependency (it is defined with the notation of BGS-TRACE-237 and 255). This is an implication only; H_mean is NOT proved. BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. |
| `BGS-TRACE-258` | **PROVED** | Line L2 Prop S: beta = 1 is slow at every prime q \|\| h_m with log_lambda q >= C_k + 6.1e-6 m (C_k effective, not computed); consequence: K2' gives no contradiction at m where h_m has such a prime below L_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Given the Line F spectral data (BGS-TRACE-198: some (+-2,8) family F has untwisted spectral radius rho0 >= mu* = 1.9154712; standing). [P] Fix F and k = k_F = 15 (BGS-TRACE-251), T = (1 - delta/2) 1.28359. f_k^F >= 0 is continuous with f_k(0) >= rho0^{2k}, so f_k >= rho0^{2k}/2 on [0, eps_k]^k; for beta = 1, tau_i = Tr(lambda^i)/q in [0, eps_k] for \|i\| <= I = floor(log_lambda(eps_k q/2)); the number of good blocks is min(r, 2I - k + 2) (indices cyclic mod r; referee nit, applied at integration), so the orbit average exceeds T^{2k} once I >= k/2 + r (T/rho0)^{2k}; with C_k = log_lambda(2/eps_k) + k/2 + 1, (1.28359/1.9154712)^30 = 6.09e-6 <= 6.1e-6 (Lean Bgs.LineL.propS_const) and r <= m, beta = 1 is slow at every prime q \|\| h_m with log_lambda q >= C_k + 6.1e-6 m. Consequence [P] (L2-F4 wording): for every m in S+ such that h_m has a prime q \|\| h_m in (max(c_W p^2, lambda^{C_k + 6.1e-6 m}), L_m), the conclusion of K2' (BGS-TRACE-246) holds without assuming frozen R', so K2' gives no contradiction at such m: K2' is true but not a refutation route there. C_k is not computed, so which m qualify is not determined by Prop S alone; the earlier [H] caveat (that this covers all or most m) is superseded by Theorem N3 (BGS-TRACE-283) [P]: its composite-conductor extension shows that c = h_p (open for the (+-2,8) families; slow orbit in (+-2,8)V) carries the slow beta = 1 for every p >= p0 (effective, not computed), so (Corollary N3', BGS-TRACE-284) the conclusion of K2' holds without assuming frozen R' at every such m in S+. The dependency on BGS-TRACE-283 is annotational only: Prop S itself does not use N3 (owner audit of PR #65, S3). |
| `BGS-TRACE-259` | **PROVED** | Line L2 Lemma A1 (exact second moment mod q): sum_{beta in l(q)} \|Zhat_beta\|^2 = q sum_u \|A(u + q)\|^2, \|D_q\|^2 <= (1 - 1/q) C_q, C_q = 1 + sum_{alpha_v in q minus 0} R(v) - \|Z\|^2/q | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255, q \|\| h_m split. [P] sum_{beta in l(q)} \|Zhat_beta\|^2 = q sum_{u in O/q} \|A(u + q)\|^2 (Parseval on O/q); by Cauchy-Schwarz over the q - 1 nonzero beta, \|D_q\|^2 <= ((q-1)/q^2) sum_{beta in l(q) minus 0} \|Zhat_beta\|^2 = (1 - 1/q) C_q with C_q := sum_u \|A(u + q)\|^2 - \|Z\|^2/q >= 0. In collision form sum_u \|A(u + q)\|^2 = sum_{v: alpha_v in q} R(v); exact collisions alpha_v = 0, v != 0 contribute 0 (Theorem U), v = 0 contributes sum \|w\|^2 = 1, so C_q = 1 + sum_{alpha_v in q minus 0} R(v) - \|Z\|^2/q exactly: only sporadic collisions remain. |
| `BGS-TRACE-260` | **PROVED** | Line L2 Prop A2 (phase-blind barrier): every cancellation-free collision bound for C_q + \|Z\|^2/q is >= G^{2m}/q, G = sum_n \|J_n(1.8)\| = 2.3693986; it certifies the target only for q >= 3.4074^m ((+-2,8)) or 2.5832^m ((+-2,16)), above h_m itself | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). [P] M(u) := sum_{n: alpha_n == u (q)} \|w(n)\|, sum_u M(u) = G^m; any bound of C_q + \|Z\|^2/q by a sum of \|w(n)\|\|w(n')\| over the pairs colliding mod q is >= sum_u M(u)^2 >= G^{2m}/q (Cauchy-Schwarz). So a phase-blind bound certifies \|D_q\| <= T^m only if G^{2m}/q <= T^{2m}, i.e. q >= (G/T)^{2m} = 3.4074^m (T = 1.28359) or 2.5832^m (T = 1.4742). [CA] G = 2.3693986 and the two ratios (barrier_consts.py; Lean Bgs.LineL barrier8, barrier16 from G >= 2.3693985). L2-F1 (applied at integration): h_m ~ (1/2) 2.4142^m, so sqrt h_m ~ 1.5538^m; both thresholds exceed h_m itself (Lean barrier_above_h, barrier_above_h21), so NO level q dividing h_m qualifies. Also [CA] the main term \|Z\|^2/q exceeds T^{2m} for every q < (1/4)(mu*/T)^{2m} = (1/4) 2.2269^m, so any usable bound is a signed statement. Notes only [N]: the exact-collision part of the absolute sum is <= C r_abs^m, r_abs = 1.0511. |
| `BGS-TRACE-261` | **PROVED** | Line L2 Lemmas B1-B3 (height/norm, split q): q <= 1.4571 lambda^{r-1} \|\|F\|\|_inf^2 for sporadic folded collisions; Minkowski vectors with \|\|F\|\|_inf <= q^{1/r}; the sum over prime ideals above h | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Positions are cyclic (lambda^m == 1 mod 2h_m); modulo q, lambda has order r in {p, 3p} (BGS-TRACE-230), and a word difference folds to F_v(X) = sum_{s<r} (sum_{t: s+tr<m} v_{s+tr}) X^s. B1, B2 are stated for SPLIT q (q == +-1 mod 8, the case q \|\| h_m); for inert q the lattice has index q^2 and B2 gives \|\|F\|\|_inf <= q^{2/r}. B1 [P]: F in Z^r, F(lambda) in q minus 0 => q <= \|N(F(lambda))\| <= (lambda/(lambda-1))^2 lambda^{r-1} \|\|F\|\|_inf^2 <= 1.4571 lambda^{r-1} \|\|F\|\|_inf^2 (r odd, G = lambda^{-c} F(lambda), c = (r-1)/2, both embeddings; (lambda/(lambda-1))^2 = 1.45711; Lean B1_const). B2 [P]: Lambda_q = {F in Z^r : F(l) = 0 in F_q} has index q, so it contains F != 0 with \|\|F\|\|_inf <= q^{1/r} (Minkowski, cube); for q < L_m and r >= p: q^{1/r} < (1.4923^3)^{1+eta} = 3.3233^{1+eta} (Lean B2_const). L2-F2 (applied at integration): 'sporadic collisions of height <= 4 exist' requires 3.3233^{1+eta} < 5, i.e. eta < 0.340, so it holds for large p only; such F is genuinely sporadic: a nonzero F with \|\|F\|\|_inf <= 4 cannot vanish at lambda (Cauchy root bound: every root has modulus < 1 + 4 = 5 < lambda = 5.83; Lean lambda_gt_five), so F(lambda) in q minus 0. B3 [P]: for alpha_v != 0, \|\|v\|\|_inf <= H, the distinct prime ideals q \| h containing alpha_v are coprime, so sum log Nq <= log \|N(alpha_v)\| <= log(1.457 lambda^{m-1} H^2), and summing with the absolute weights gives sum_{q \| h} log Nq (Coll^abs_q - exact part) <= (1 + o(1)) m log lambda G^{2m}. [assessment, not registered] B3 does not help (it is compatible with Prop A2). |
| `BGS-TRACE-262` | **PROVED** | Line L2: for all sufficiently large p the height zone of Lemma B1 and the open range (c_W p^2, L_m) are disjoint, for (+-2,8) and (+-2,16) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231 and BGS-TRACE-243 (S+, O-natural_m, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q); targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). [P] With r >= p the height argument (B1) excludes sporadic collisions only for q > 1.457 lambda^{p-1} H^2, growth lambda^{m/3} = 1.7996^m (r = 3p: lambda^m, worse). (+-2,8): log L_m/m = 0.40030 (1 + eta_m) < log(lambda)/3 = 0.58758 iff eta_m < 0.4678, true for all large p since eta_m <= (2.010 + o(1))/log(2p+1) (BGS-TRACE-238). (+-2,16): Lambda_16 (1 + eta^(16)) < 0.58758 iff eta^(16) < 3.77 (3.767 with the exact Lambda_16 = 0.12326; 3.770 with the quoted 0.12319), true for all sufficiently large p since eta^(16) <= (4.282 + o(1))/log(2p+1) (BGS-TRACE-253); L2-F3 (applied at integration): 'for every p' replaced by 'for all sufficiently large p' (the bounds carry o(1) and O(log l/m) terms). Hence for large p the height zone does not meet (c_W p^2, L_m), and the height argument closes neither the (+-2,16) range nor any part of the (+-2,8) range. |
| `BGS-TRACE-263` | **PROVED** | Line L3 S1 (walk-measure form): A = chi B_m with chi(x) = e(-nu0 y' Nm x/2h) and Bhat_m(y) = prod_{j<m} g(Tr lambda^j y), g(t) = e^{i 1.8 cos 2 pi t}, so \|Bhat_m\| = 1 | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206; m = 3p, notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255 (w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), A, Zhat_beta, D_q, l(q)); T = 1.28359 for (+-2,8). [P] By BGS-TRACE-212, P(n) = y' Nm(alpha_n) - 2h sum_{i,j} y_{\|i-j\|} n_i n_j (sum x_{\|i-j\|} n_i n_j = alpha_n alphabar_n, Tr lambda^d = 2 x_d), and the 2h-term gives e(integer) = 1. Hence w(n) = Phihat(n) chi(alpha_n), chi(x) = e(-nu0 y' Nm x/2h) is 2hO-periodic, A(x) = chi(x) B_m(x) with B_m(x) = sum_{alpha_n = x} Phihat(n) (the law of the walk alpha_n with i.i.d. complex steps ghat(n_j) at positions lambda^j), and Bhat_m(y) = sum_x B_m(x) e(Tr xy) = prod_{j<m} g(Tr lambda^j y) with g(t) = sum ghat(n) e(nt) = e^{i 1.8 cos 2 pi t} (Jacobi-Anger), so \|Bhat_m\| = 1 identically (the Fourier side of Theorem U). |
| `BGS-TRACE-264` | **PROVED** | Line L3 S2 (CRT split): A(q) is a level-2h/q Gauss sum of B_m on q; \|A(q)\| <= h/q and \|D_q\| <= 2h/q when \|Z\| <= h, which gives \|D_q\| <= T^m only for q >~ 1.8808^m, above L_m | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206; m = 3p, notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255 (w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), A, Zhat_beta, D_q, l(q)); T = 1.28359 for (+-2,8). [P] q \|\| h an odd prime, M' = 2h/q (even, == 2 mod 4), a M' + b q = 1 (gcd(q, M') = 1): 1/2h = a/q + b/M', chi(x) = e(-c a Nm x/q) e(-c b Nm x/M'), c = nu0 y'; Nm x == 0 (mod q) on q u qbar, so A(q) = sum_{x in q} B_m(x) psi'(x) with psi' at level M'; Fourier on both factors (characters indexed by O^dual, or (Z/M')^2, since the trace pairing degenerates at 2): A(q) = sum_z Fhat(z) Bhat_m(z/2h), F = 1_q tensor psi', sum \|Fhat_q\| = 1. L3-F1 (applied at integration): \|Fhat_{M'}\| = 2/M' on exactly M'^2/4 points and 0 elsewhere (referee check with the actual coefficient at (m, q) = (9, 199), (9, 7)), so sum \|Fhat_{M'}\| = M'/2 and, with \|Bhat_m\| = 1 (BGS-TRACE-263), \|A(q)\| <= h/q and \|D_q\| <= h/q + \|Z\|/q <= 2h/q whenever \|Z\| <= h (true for m >= 495 by the Z-bound of Prop K2, BGS-TRACE-238, \|Z_rho\| <= C_rho mu^m, C ~ 1/2, mu = 1.9154753 < 1 + sqrt2; for smaller m to be checked case by case). Hence \|D_q\| <= T^m follows only for q >= 2h/T^m ~ ((1 + sqrt2)/1.28359)^m = 1.8808^m (Lean L3_F1_rate), which is above L_m = 1.4923^{m(1+eta_m)} once eta_m < 0.578 (all large p). This is the large-sieve threshold of REPORT_L_v2 section 5(c) as an exact trivial bound (the author's 4h/q, threshold 2 x 1.8808^m, sharpened by L3-F1). |
| `BGS-TRACE-265` | **PROVED** | Line L3 S3 (no Gauss-side folding): h_{3p} = h_p (4 h_p^2 + 3); every prime of K_p = 4 h_p^2 + 3 other than 7 (rank 3) has rank 3p, so the level-K_p factor of the m = 3p phase does not reduce to m = p | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206; m = 3p, notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255 (w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), A, Zhat_beta, D_q, l(q)); T = 1.28359 for (+-2,8). But h_{3p} = h_p (4 h_p^2 + 3) (V_{3n} = V_n^3 + 3 V_n for odd n, V_n = eta^n - eta^-n = 2 h_n, eta = 1 + sqrt2) and gcd(h_p, K_p) \| 3, K_p = 4 h_p^2 + 3. L3-F2 (applied at integration): if q' \| K_p and lambda^p == 1 mod q' then q' divides 2h_1 = 2 or 2h_3 = 14, so the only exception is 7, of rank 3 (not 3 as the author wrote); 7 \| K_p for every p = 5..23 (49 \| K_7, so 49 \| h_21; v_7(h_{3p}) = 1 + v_7(p)), 3 never occurs (gcd = 1 for p <= 23); every other prime of K_p has rank 3p (e.g. 31, 4663, 313, 4088137, 409, 457, 14593). By S2 (BGS-TRACE-264) the phase psi' has a factor at level K_p, where lambda^p is not == 1 and no folding is available: the Gauss side does not reduce to m = p. Exact checks for p <= 23 (lineL_checks.py; referee; Lean L3_S3_identity, L3_F2_seven, L3_F2_49 for p = 5..23). (49 \| h_21 does not affect '49 does not divide h' in Prop K2, which is for m >= 495.) |

| `BGS-TRACE-267` | **PROVED** | Line L3 Lemma Q (exact line quadrature): (1/q) sum_{beta in l(q)} F(beta/q) = sum_{gamma in q} c_gamma; so the isotropic-line mean block energy lies in [I_k - T_k(q), I_k + T_k(q)] for every q, without Weil | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206; m = 3p, notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255 (w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), A, Zhat_beta, D_q, l(q)); T = 1.28359 for (+-2,8). Use: for F = f_k (BGS-TRACE-243 notation) the isotropic-line mean block energy lies in [I_k - T_k(q), I_k + T_k(q)], with no Weil input and no upper limit on q. | `BGS-TRACE-268` | **PROVED** | Line L3 Lemma E (cusp excursions): if dist(Tr(beta lambda^j)/q, Z) <= eps for k >= 3 consecutive j (0 < eps <= 1/16, beta != 0), then lambda^{k-1} <= 264 eps^2 q (beta isotropic) and <= 264 eps^2 q^2 (every beta != 0) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineL_op_open_range.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2, frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206; m = 3p, notation of BGS-TRACE-254, 255 (w(n) = Phihat(n) e(-nu0 P(n)/2h), A, Zhat_beta, D_q, l(q)); T = 1.28359 for (+-2,8). The bound with q^2 holds for every beta != 0 (referee). At eps = 0.05: isotropic runs <= log_lambda q + 0.76, others <= 2 log_lambda q + 0.76. [N] excursion_check.py: observed runs within the bounds at q = 41, 239, 353, 599, 4663, 15607. [assessment, not registered] Lemma E does not control time near other rational points. |
| `BGS-TRACE-269` | **PROVED** | Line M M1: gcd(h_m, h_n) = h_gcd(m,n) for odd m, n; for m = 3p every prime q != 7 dividing h_m has r(q) in {p, 3p}, so the open conductors at distinct m = 3p are pairwise disjoint (only 7 is shared) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). [P] By the r(q) lemma (BGS-TRACE-230, credited; eps^{2m} - 1 = 2 h_m eps^m for odd m) q \| h_m iff r(q) \| m for odd primes q; by lifting the exponent in O/q^k (q odd, unramified in Z[sqrt2]) v_q(h_m) = v_q(h_r) + v_q(m/r) for r = r(q) \| m, and 2 does not divide h_m; hence gcd(h_m, h_n) = h_gcd(m,n) for odd m, n. Corollary: on m = 3p every prime q != 7 dividing h_m has r(q) in {p, 3p}, so open conductors at distinct m = 3p are pairwise disjoint and only 7 is shared (exception of BGS-TRACE-230: 49 \| h_21, p = 7; 49 does not divide h_{3p} for p != 7). Checks: [N] 0 failures for odd m, n <= 301 (author), m, n <= 601 with LTE checks (referee); Lean Bgs.LineM.strong_div_small (odd m, n < 60), nsw_three_mul (h_{3m} = h_m (4 h_m^2 + 3), general), three_not_dvd_nsw. |
| `BGS-TRACE-270` | **PROVED** | Line M M2 (averaging lemma): a positive-weight averaged form of H-natural (with delta) implies H-natural with delta/2; averaged hypotheses are never weaker | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). D7~_m := phi^-1 D7_m >= 0.99 K_j 1.0184224^m > 0 on S+ for m >= M0(j) (the proved [CA] parts of Theorem J3, BGS-TRACE-225, 227; S+ infinite, BGS-TRACE-235), O~_m := phi^-1 O-natural_m (one normalisation). [P] Let c_m >= 0 on S+ with sum_m c_m D7~_m = infinity. If for some delta in (0,1) sum_{m <= M} c_m (O~_m + (1 - delta) D7~_m) >= 0 for infinitely many M, then O~_m >= -(1 - delta/2) D7~_m for infinitely many m in S+ (otherwise the partial sums tend to -infinity). H-natural (BGS-TRACE-232, open) is the conclusion's form only, cited by reference; it is not a dependency (it is neither assumed nor proved here). Notes only [assessment, not registered]: the signed-weight remark and 'no Tauberian argument from R' and the linear D7 data alone'. |

(b) Its conductor-h_p part is open in the sense of BGS-TRACE-246 (not in {1, 7} u {q, 7q : q <= c_W p^2}, not absorbed); v1's 'h_p < L_m < sqrt h' is dropped (referee M-F5d). (c) Dev_{h_p} >= -n_{h_p} \|Z_rho\|^2/h_p^2 is the Prop K2 per-level bound (BGS-TRACE-238); n_q = q - 2 for q \|\| h (BGS-TRACE-228), every q \| h_p is == 1 mod 2p (BGS-TRACE-230), omega(h_p) <= log h_p/log(2p+1), so n_{h_p}/h_p >= 1 - 2 omega(h_p)/(2p+1) -> 1 ([N] p <= 61: all h_p squarefree, ratio in [0.908, 1]); with the Line F F3 bound \|Z_rho\| >= (1/2)(1 - lambda^-m)(mu_lo^m - 3012.4), mu_lo = 1.9154712 (BGS-TRACE-198, 199) and phi <= h, the floor in units of phi is >= c (mu_lo^2/lambda^{2/3})^m >= c 1.13287^m (mu_lo^2/lambda^{2/3} = 1.1328794), exceeding the D7 demand 0.99 K_j 1.0184224^m (BGS-TRACE-225) by more than 1.11238^m (ratio 1.1123866) [constant arithmetic; lineMN_checks.py; the report's 1.13288 and 1.11239 are rounded to nearest and are registered here as the lower bounds 1.13287, 1.11238]. (d) Meaning: no positivity or Cauchy-Schwarz bound at d = h_p can force the cross part there above the D7 demand: a no-go for the METHOD, not a refutation, and it does not show that a conspiracy is realisable. (e) Prime factors q \| h_p carry the same capacity statement but need not be open (23 \| h_11, 47 \| h_23, 79 \| h_13 lie in the Weil range for large p). Not registered: 'every refutation needs a signed equidistribution theorem at h_p' ([assessment]). BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. |

Any included level d (prime or composite). [P] With X_nu := Z(nu) - Z_rho/d for the members and S := sum_{nu in M_d} X_nu, Dev_d = 2 Re(conj Z_rho S)/d + sum \|X_nu\|^2 (pure algebra: the G3 main term is exactly Z_rho/d; G3/K1, BGS-TRACE-237, is used only to name S = D_iso - E_b/q at prime q \|\| h). Cauchy-Schwarz: sum \|X_nu\|^2 >= \|S\|^2/n_d; a cross value -X needs \|S\| >= dX/(2\|Z_rho\|), so sq_d >= d^2 X^2/(4 \|Z_rho\|^2 n_d) ~ d X^2/(4 \|Z_rho\|^2), which offsets X only if d >~ 4\|Z_rho\|^2/X (with X ~ phi 1.0184^m this is the base mu*^2/xi_lo = 1.4923 of L_m). Minimising over S gives exactly -n_d \|Z_rho\|^2/d^2, the per-level bound of Prop K2 (BGS-TRACE-238); 'sharp' means within this quadratic relaxation (equality when all X_nu are equal); any Cauchy-Schwarz lower bound with a test vector reduces to first order to the same linear functional S. Referee check on random data: max relative error 4e-14. Registered as a refinement of BGS-TRACE-238 (annotation there). |

| `BGS-TRACE-277` | **PROVED** | Line M M7a (arithmetic): ord_c(lambda) \| 3p for c \| h_{3p}; lambda^p = 1 + 2 h_p eps^p, so lambda^p == 1 mod 2h_p; p = 2 log_lambda h_p + 2 log_lambda 2 + o(1); open prime conductors q \| h_{3p} have ord_q(lambda) <= 3p < q | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Math. [P] f_k^F(y) = \|\|P_k^F(y)\|\|_HS^2 = sum_gamma c_gamma e(Tr gamma y), sum_{gamma != 0} \|c_gamma\| <= A_k (BGS-TRACE-250); (1/r) sum_{i<r} f_k(beta lambda^i/q) = I_k + sum_{gamma != 0} c_gamma S(gamma beta), S(x) = (1/r) sum_{h in H} e((sigma(x) h + sigmabar(x)/h)/q), H = <sigma(lambda)> <= F_q^*, r in {p, 3p} (BGS-TRACE-230, 243); every q \| h_{3p}, q != 7, is == +-1 mod 8 (split) and == 1 mod 2p. The chirp, the sector phases and ghat(n) are absorbed into c_gamma (OP_abs does not use them): no quadratic phase remains. S(x) is the Ostafe-Shparlinski-Voloch sum S_{2,q}(a, b; A) with A = diag(l, 1/l); non-isotropic x is OSV's independence case, isotropic x gives a Gauss period. 7q conductors: 3 cosets of a subgroup of order p, so every bound applies with tau = p. Budget: Theorem W (BGS-TRACE-245) needs A_k sup_{x nondeg} \|S(x)\| <= eta_F/3, i.e. sup_{x nondeg} \|S(x)\| <= eps_F := eta_F/(3 A_k^F) (owner audit of PR #65, R3) (Line L convention, reproducing c_W = 10^-25.615 from the Weil bound BGS-TRACE-241) and T_k(q) <= eta for q >= q0 (BGS-TRACE-252), for m >= m1 (effective, not computed; N-F7); eps_F = 10^-12.51 (8V), 10^-10.17 (8E), 10^-10.96 (16V), 10^-10.12 (16E); the conductor count tau(h_m) = e^{O(m/log m)} is absorbed by (1 - delta_F/2)^m, so eps_F does not depend on m or on D7. Referee recomputation identical (ref_n.py). |
| `BGS-TRACE-279` | **PROVED** | Line N N1.1-N1.3: moment inequality \|S\|^{2kl} <= q^2 tau^{2kl-2k-2l} J_k J_l, energies J_2 <= 2 tau^2 - tau and J_3 <= 17.31 tau^{11/3} (q >= c_W p^2, tau >= 10^45), and the explicit binomial bounds (2,2), (2,3), (3,3) with the three-branch isotropic bound (Gauss period and both Cochrane-Pinner branches) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). q prime, H' a subgroup or coset of <lambda mod q> of odd order tau >= p. N1.1 [P] (OSV (6.6) re-derived in diagonal form): \|S(a)\|^{2kl} <= q^2 tau^{2kl-2k-2l} J_k J_l for a1 a2 != 0. N1.2(a) [P] J_2 <= 2 tau^2 - tau (-1 not in H for tau odd; equality at all 17 tested levels [N]). N1.2(b) [P + P-cite] J_3 <= tau^3 [4 3^{4/3} (q tau/(q-1))^{2/3} + 3 q tau^2/(q-1)^2] + 12 tau^3: degenerate (a,b) (ab(ab-1) = 0) contribute <= 12 tau^3; for non-degenerate (a,b) the curve (X^s+Y^s+a)(X^s+Y^s+bX^sY^s) - X^sY^s, s = (q-1)/tau, is absolutely irreducible (OSV IMRN 2023 Lemma 4.3, EXT-OSV-2023) of degree 3s < q and has <= 4 d^{4/3} q^{2/3} + 3q points (Voloch, Acta Arith. 52 (1989), cited via OSV Math. Proc. Camb. 176 (2024) Lemma 2.1, EXT-OSV-2024; the original was not retrieved); hypothesis restated by N-F4: for q >= c_W p^2 and tau >= 10^45 this gives J_3 <= 17.31 tau^{11/3} (4 3^{4/3} = 17.30699; referee-verified over the whole Theorem W+ range; the author's 'q >= tau^2' fails there). N1.3 [P + P-cite] non-isotropic x: (2,2) \|S\|/tau <= (2q/tau^2)^{1/4} (never beats Weil); (2,3) \|S\|/tau <= 34.62^{1/12} q^{1/6} tau^{-13/36}; (3,3) \|S\|/tau <= 17.31^{1/9} (q/tau^{7/3})^{1/9} ((2,3), (3,3) under the J_3 hypothesis). Isotropic x (owner audit of PR #65, R1/R2): \|S\|/tau <= min(sqrt q/tau, lambda q^{1/4} tau^{-5/8}, lambda q^{1/8} tau^{-3/8}), lambda = 2/3^{1/4} = 1.519671 (the report's 1.51967 is rounded down; the isotropic range constant 10^-101.5 is unaffected at that precision), each branch used only in its Cochrane-Pinner range (Q. J. Math. 62 (2011) Thm 1.1, EXT-CP-2011): sqrt q (Gauss period) for every tau; lambda tau^{3/8} q^{1/4} for q^{1/2} < tau <= q^{2/3}/3; lambda tau^{5/8} q^{1/8} for 3 q^{1/3} < tau <= q^{1/2} (ranges checked against the author preprint of 5 Oct 2009; the published QJM text was not accessible). A coset gA has the same bounds (replace a by ag). The middle branch is needed: for c_W p^2 < q < tau^2 the 5/8 branch does not apply and sqrt q/tau exceeds eps_F once q > eps_F^2 tau^2, which happens inside the Theorem W+ range for p >= 10^266.4 (tau = p). With all three branches the isotropic case is covered on the whole Theorem W+ range for every p, with no upper limit on p: analytic case split (Gauss for q <= eps_F^2 tau^2; middle branch for eps_F^2 tau^2 < q < tau^2, valid since tau >= p >= 10^101.6; 5/8 branch for tau^2 <= q <= c_N p^{7/3}) plus a grid check for 10^264.5 <= p <= 10^400 and larger p (iso_coverage.py, all four families). Integration note I-1. [N] all three bounds hold with room on 17 real levels q \| h_{3p}, q <= 6000 (n3_check.py; referee direct count). |

Standing inputs and m1 exactly as in Theorem W (BGS-TRACE-245; m >= m1 effective, not computed; no explicit p0 is claimed). (2,3) gives 10^-75.8 p^{13/6} and is never best once (3,3) beats Weil; isotropic x is covered on the whole range for every p by the three-branch bound of N1.3 (sqrt q for q <= eps_F^2 tau^2, the middle Cochrane-Pinner branch lambda q^{1/4} tau^{-5/8} for eps_F^2 tau^2 < q < tau^2, the 5/8 branch for tau^2 <= q <= c_N p^{7/3}; owner audit of PR #65, R1; analytic case split and grid in iso_coverage.py); the 5/8 branch alone would extend to 10^-101.5 p^3, but without the middle branch the range c_W p^2 < q < tau^2 is not covered for p >= 10^266.4. Nothing for q > p^C for every fixed C, nor for composite conductors (BGS-TRACE-282, open). |

| `BGS-TRACE-283` | **PROVED** | Line N Theorem N3 (scarred conductors carry slow beta): for F = (+-2,8)V, theta_F = 5.39e-6 and t_F effective, not computed, every conductor c with r = ord_c lambda > 2(N + k) and log_lambda c >= theta_F (1 + kappa) r/2 + t_F + k has the slow beta = 1; in particular c = h_p for every p >= p0 (effective, not computed) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). F = (+-2,8)V, k = k_F = 15 (BGS-TRACE-251), delta_F of BGS-TRACE-252. N3 extends L2 Prop S (BGS-TRACE-258, prime levels q \|\| h_m; credited, not a dependency) to composite conductors, chiefly h_p, with an explicit theta (N-F1). Setting: c >= 2, lambda^r == 1 mod cO, r = ord_c lambda; mu := 1.9154712 <= \|mu*\|, the certified eigenvalue of the Line F vertex operator L_{V,4} (BGS-TRACE-198 [CA], per family; N-F2; the nonzero spectrum of these trace-class operators is fixed by their traces, which do not depend on the disc radius or the beta-weight conjugation, REPORT_L_v2 section 2), so rho(L_0^F) >= mu; theta_F := ((1 - delta_F/2) target_F/mu)^{2k} = 5.39e-6 (n6_theta.py; Lean Bgs.LineN.theta8V: < 5.4e-6). [P] There is t_F = O(k), effective but not computed (rough estimate t_F ~ 25 + k ~ 40 [H, referee], N-F3), such that if r > 2(N + k) with N := log_lambda c - t_F and log_lambda c >= theta_F (1 + kappa) r/2 + t_F + k for some kappa > 0, then (1/r) sum_{i<r} f_k^F(lambda^i/c) > ((1 - delta_F/2) target_F)^{2k}: Tr(lambda^n)/c is r-periodic; for \|n\| <= N the twists are <= 2 lambda^-t and the 2(N - k) + 1 fully small blocks are distinct mod r; f_k is a Fourier series with sum \|c_gamma\| (\|sigma gamma\| + \|sigmabar gamma\|) < infinity (OP_abs, BGS-TRACE-250), so continuity gives f_k >= (1 - kappa') f_k(0) on them; f_k(0) = \|\|L_0^k\|\|_HS^2 >= mu^{2k}; f_k >= 0. Consequences: c = h_p (r = p, log_lambda h_p = p/2 - O(1) from lambda^p - 1 = 2 h_p eps^p, BGS-TRACE-277) is slow for every p >= p0 (effective, not computed; with t_F ~ 40 roughly p >~ 110 [H]); so is every prime q \| K_p with q >= lambda^{(8.1e-6 + o(1)) p + t_F} and every q \| h_p with q >= lambda^{(2.7e-6 + o(1)) p + t_F}. Integration note I-3: only F = (+-2,8)V is used; the (8E) value 6.01e-6 is not registered (BGS-TRACE-198 places the spectrum of L_E in \|z\| < 1). Compatibility (referee): Theorem U, Lemma E (BGS-TRACE-268), H_mean (a mean over beta) and Theorem W are not contradicted. Not a refutation. BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. |
| `BGS-TRACE-284` | **PROVED** | Line N Corollary N3': for every m = 3p in S+ with p >= p0 (effective, not computed) the conclusion of K2' holds without assuming frozen R' (c = h_p is open for the (+-2,8) families and carries the slow beta = 1 in (+-2,8)V), so K2' is never a refutation route there and the unweighted 'exclude slow lambda-orbits' target is false | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). [P on BGS-TRACE-198 [CA]] For m = 3p in S+ with p >= p0 (effective, not computed; N-F3) and m >= 495, c = h_p is in the open set of K2' (open for the (+-2,8) families; slow orbit in (+-2,8)V) (BGS-TRACE-246, by M3, BGS-TRACE-271) and carries the slow beta = 1 (Theorem N3, BGS-TRACE-283). Hence the conclusion of K2' ('frozen R' implies some open conductor carries a slow lambda-orbit') holds without assuming frozen R', and no strengthening of Theorem W (Weil, OSV, Bourgain, or any bound for all primes < L_m) can make the conclusion of K2' (BGS-TRACE-246) yield a contradiction for any such m; Theorem U (BGS-TRACE-254) and Lemma E (BGS-TRACE-268) are not contradicted (owner audit of PR #65, S2): at c = h_p the same computation gives \|S(1)\| >= (1 - O(t/p)) r, no cancellation. The 'remaining obstruction' of REPORT_L_v2 section 6 / BGS-TRACE-247 annotation ('exclude slow lambda-orbits at conductors c in (c_W p^2, L_m)') is false on c >= lambda^{theta r/2 + t} (theta = theta_(+-2,8)V = 5.39e-6, t = t_F of BGS-TRACE-283); it must be replaced by a beta-weighted statement (H_mean, BGS-TRACE-256, is such a mean statement). [N, notes only] K2 single-disc toy rates at c = h_p (n5_slow_toy.py). BGS-TRACE-206 (frozen R') is NOT refuted. |
| `BGS-TRACE-285` | **PROVED** | Line N N4 (identification of the inherited level): the conductor-h_p points ((1/h_p)O + O^dual)/O^dual (h_p^2 points) form a subgroup of index 4 in Fix(T^p) (4 h_p^2 points), so level-h_p twisted sums are periodic-orbit sums of the cat map | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineMN_conductor_structure.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Torus T = (O tensor R)/O^dual. [P] Fix(T^p) = (lambda^p - 1)^-1 O^dual/O^dual = (4 sqrt2 h_p eps^p)^-1 O/O^dual has \|det(S^p - 1)\| = 4 h_p^2 points (lambda^p - 1 = 2 h_p eps^p, BGS-TRACE-277); the conductor-h_p points ((1/h_p)O + O^dual)/O^dual have h_p^2 points, all fixed, so they form a subgroup of index 4 = N(4 sqrt2)/[O^dual : O] = 32/8 (N-F8: the author's '32' omitted [O^dual : O] = N(2 sqrt2) = 8). Hence, with L3 S4 (BGS-TRACE-266), the twisted sums at level h_p are twisted traces Tr(M_y^3) along period-p orbits, M_y = prod_{i<p} L_{tau(T^i y)}, and up to the bounded index 4 the sum over beta is a sum over all period-p periodic points. Checks: brute force p = 3, 5, 7 (#Fix = 196, 6724, 228484; referee), Lean Bgs.LineN.fix3, fix5, fix7 (\|det(S^p - 1)\| = 4 h_p^2) and idx (32/8 = 4). Notes only [H, not registered]: the weighted margin (mu*^3/lambda)^{1/3} = 1.0644 and the pressure target limsup (1/p) log sum_{y in Fix T^p} \|Tr M_y^3\| < 2.512. |

(4) Bowen-ball lemma: Fix(A^p) is (p, eps)-separated by expansivity, so for continuous phi, sum_{y in G_p} e^{S_p phi(y)} <= (lambda^{p+k}/(c' eps^2)) e^{p omega_phi(eps)} int e^{S_p phi} dLeb. | `BGS-TRACE-291` | **PROVED** | Line N3 Lemma 1.1 (geometric twist sequences): for prime q = qq' split, q \| h_p, the isotropic beta in the ideal q have tau_i(beta) = s l^i / q mod 1 with l = lambda mod qbar of order exactly p; unit beta have tau_i = (s1 l^i + s2 l^-i)/q | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN2N4_inherited_level.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Setting: q prime, q \|\| h_m, q \| h_p, m = 3p, q = qq' split in O = Z[sqrt2]; l := lambda mod q' in F_q^*. [P] l has order exactly p: it divides p since lambda^p - 1 = 2 h_p eps^p (BGS-TRACE-277), and is not 1 since N(lambda - 1) = -4 (q odd). For beta in q minus 0 (mod q), y := beta/q in q'^{-1}/O and gamma -> Tr(gamma y) mod 1 factors through O/q' = F_q (y q' in O, Tr(O) in Z), so tau_i(beta) = Tr(lambda^i y) = s l^i / q (mod 1) for a unique s in F_q^*; on the other line the ratio is l^{-1} (N(lambda) = 1). For unit beta, tau_i = (s1 l^i + s2 l^{-i})/q with s1 s2 != 0. So the isotropic points are exactly those whose twist sequence is one geometric progression, and Zhat_beta is constant on <l>-cosets ((q - 1)/p orbits per line; cyclicity of the trace). [N] check: 4 distinct values per line at (15, 41), 17 at (21, 239). |
| `BGS-TRACE-292` | **PROVED** | Line N3 Lemma 1.2 (isotropic part as a line quadrature): at a prime q \|\| h, q \| h_p, the isotropic cross part is 2 Re(conj Z_rho ((q - 2)/q)(D_q + D_qbar)), the same weight for +-rho | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN2N4_inherited_level.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). Setting of BGS-TRACE-291. [P] The J3 weight on isotropic beta != 0 is the real constant (q - 2)/q^2 (BGS-TRACE-229), the same for +-rho. By orthogonality on the line l(q), sum_{beta in l(q) minus 0} Zhat_beta = q D (TRACE-255 notation: D_q := (1/q) sum_{l(q) minus 0} Zhat for the line l(q); N3 writes this q D_qbar, N3-F3; only the sum over both lines is used), the two lines meeting only at 0. Hence the isotropic cross part at level q is 2 Re(conj Z_rho ((q - 2)/q)(D_q + D_qbar)). The trivial bound \|D_q\| <= 4h/q gives rate lambda^{1/3} > T (consistent with BGS-TRACE-264); this lemma does not bound D_q. |
| `BGS-TRACE-293` | **PROVED** | Line N3 M6 condition C3: W_{q,beta}(-rho) = conj W_{q,beta}(rho) at every prime level q \|\| h_m for rho = (+-2,8), and at composite levels via inheritance (isotropic weights equal) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN2N4_inherited_level.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). rho = (+-2,8). [P] From 8 nu0 == 2 sg (mod 2h) (h odd), nu0(-rho) == -nu0(rho) (mod q); the members are nu = nu0 + (2h/q) j with labels t = j y' and y' independent of rho, so the missing label t_b (nu == 0 mod q) satisfies t_b(-rho) = -t_b(rho) and W_{q,beta}(-rho) = q^-2(-1 - e(-N(beta) tbar_b/q)) = conj W_{q,beta}(rho) (BGS-TRACE-229); in particular the isotropic weights (q - 2)/q^2 are equal. This agrees with the d = 7 statement of BGS-TRACE-221(ii). Composite levels follow from BGS-TRACE-228 since n_e does not depend on the sign. This proves condition C3 of BGS-TRACE-276 (C1 is the widened BGS-TRACE-222; C2 stays open, see BGS-TRACE-276). |

E: two steps each way, rounding offset <= 1/(2(lambda - 1)) = 0.1036, theta <= 0.635, exponent -0.3 + 2.5/lambda^2 < 0. 16V (Lemma I16): theta <= (1/2 + 1.2/lambda)/0.9 = 0.784, F -> O1 theta <= 0.510, exponent -0.226 < 0; the multiplier sup is e^{2 pi (c0/2)\|n\| rho} and the internal 2 x 2 factor is unitary. 16E as 8E. Letter sums converge against \|J_n(1.8)\|; the twist enters as the unimodular e(n tau), so all bounds are uniform in tau. Integration note I-1: the 16V core bound f = 2.94138957 plus penalty 3.9e-8 is 2.94138961 > 2.9413896 (the author's and referee's rounded value), so the registered bound is 2.9413897 (Lean Bgs.LineN4.lam16V_rounding, assembly). The (-) families: for (+-2,8), RK (BGS-TRACE-222, widened; scoped to (+-2,8)) is an isometry of H(rho, beta) mapping (C, a, tau) -> (-C, a - 4, tau) letterwise, so Lambda_free^{(-2,8)} = Lambda_free^{(+2,8)} (N3-F6). For (+-2,16), BGS-TRACE-222 is not used: the Schur certificate is sign-independent (the majorant M = sum_n \|J_n(1.8)\| \|B_n\| (x) P_n depends only on \|C\| = c0/2 and not on the sign of a or the zeta16 phases; b16_schur.py, REPORT_N4_v2 section 2.2), so the same bounds hold for both (+2,16) and (-2,16). Integration checks: the six Schur runs rerun bit for bit with x, y stored and d = sqrt(x/y)/max exactly (N4-F8, check_schur_xy.py); exact Z[zeta16] inner products and norms of all 128 blocks (N4-F5, blocks16_exact_inner.py). Referee: independent arb spot checks, Schur rerun, cell-coverage check. Consistency: mu* <= 1.9154752 <= Lambda_free^{8V}; Line F 8E sector-4 complement radius 0.3825 <= 0.4916. Supersedes the N3 [CA*] values 1.965736 (8V) and 1.660338 (8E). |
| `BGS-TRACE-296` | **PROVED** | Line N3/N4 Theorem C': for p with h_p squarefree and p >= p0 (effective, not computed), the unit-beta cross part at c = h_p is O(((1 - delta) T_F)^m) for all four families, with unit-point rates (Lambda r_2)^{1/3} <= 1.27438 (8V), 0.80266 (8E), 1.45789 (16V), 0.77812 (16E) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN2N4_inherited_level.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). [P + CA] Statement (an implication for each p): for every prime p with h_p squarefree and p >= p0 (effective, not computed), and F in {(+-2,8)V, (+-2,8)E, (+-2,16)V, (+-2,16)E}, E_{y in G_p} \|Zhat^F(y)\| <= e^{o(p)} (Lambda^F r_2^F)^p, with (Lambda^F r_2^F)^{1/3} <= 1.27438 (8V), 0.80266 (8E) (targets 1.28359) and 1.45789 (16V), 0.77812 (16E) (target 1.4742) (Lean Bgs.LineN4.unit_rates, unit_rates_registered); hence the cross part at c = h_p restricted to unit beta (a unit at every prime of h_p) is O(((1 - delta) T_F)^m), delta >= 0.0071 for (+-2,8) and >= 0.011 for (+-2,16). Ingredients: Theorem C (BGS-TRACE-289), Lemma I / I16 (BGS-TRACE-294), Lambda certificates (BGS-TRACE-295), Prop E (BGS-TRACE-290), Theorem B (BGS-TRACE-288), unit weights \|W\| <= 2^{omega(h_p)}/h_p^2 = e^{O(p/log p)}/\|G_p\| from the prime-level J3 weights (BGS-TRACE-229) and CRT/inheritance (BGS-TRACE-228). 'h_p squarefree' is part of the statement because weights at a conductor q^a, a >= 2, are not registered (N3-F8, N4-F4); it is NOT proved for any p here: [N] evidence only, no prime q < 3e6 has q^2 \| h_p for a prime p <= 1e5 (d_squarefree.py, 2750 pairs (p, q)). Not covered: isotropic beta (BGS-TRACE-298); conductors d \| h_p with h_p/d > kappa^{p/2} (kappa = T^3/(Lambda_V r_2) = 1.0218), the unit points at the mixed level 7 h_p (N3-F9, [P-sketch], not registered), K_p-prime and mixed rank-3p conductors. At q = h_p the (+-2,16) level is already absorbed by Line L section 5(b) (BGS-TRACE-253) for large p. Frozen R' is NOT refuted. |
| `BGS-TRACE-297` | **PROVED** | Line N4 gauge lemma: L_tau T_sigma = T_{lambda sigma} L_{tau - kappa lambda sigma}, L_tau G_nu = G_{nu/lambda} L_{tau + nu/lambda}; a product along a two-sided geometric twist t0 + a lambda^i + b lambda^-i is a gauge conjugate of L_{t0}^n up to a unimodular scalar | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN2N4_inherited_level.md) | Scope: k1 kick 0.9 cos 2 pi q at eta = 2 (ghat(n) = i^n J_n(1.8)), S = [[3,4],[2,3]], lambda = 3+2 sqrt2 = eps^2 (eps = 1+sqrt2), frozen definition of BGS-TRACE-206 (P* = {(2,8),(-2,8),(2,16),(-2,16)}); m = 3p, p prime, notation of BGS-TRACE-231, 243 and 246 (h_m, S+, O-natural_m, D7_m, K_j, xi_lo = 2.4586893, mu* = 1.9154712, f_k^F, I_k^F, A_k, T_k(q), open conductors, slow beta; targets 1.28359 for (+-2,8), 1.4742 for (+-2,16)). L_tau Phi(s) = sum_n w(n) e(n tau) e(kappa n s) Phi((s + n)/lambda) on functions holomorphic near R (the lattice-of-discs space of BGS-TRACE-197 is its localisation), T_sigma Phi(s) = Phi(s + sigma), G_nu Phi(s) = e(nu s) Phi(s). [P] Direct substitution gives L_tau T_sigma = T_{lambda sigma} L_{tau - kappa lambda sigma} and L_tau G_nu = G_{nu/lambda} L_{tau + nu/lambda}. Corollary: for tau_i = t0 + a lambda^i + b lambda^{-i} (i < n), sigma_i = -a lambda^{i-1}/kappa, nu_i = b lambda^{1-i}: prod_{i<n} L_{tau_i} = T_{-lambda sigma_{n-1}} G_{-b lambda^{1-n}} L_{t0}^n G_{lambda b} T_{sigma_0} up to a unimodular scalar (T_sigma G_nu = e(nu sigma) G_nu T_sigma; N4-F1), harmless for every norm or modulus statement; on the disc space this holds whenever the gauges are below the disc margin. Isotropic twists (BGS-TRACE-291) are two-sided geometric about the sector-4 constant twist t0 = 1/2. Not registered at this integration: the single-slow-window analysis of N4 sections 3.3-3.4 (requirement G <= 1.547, leading rate 1.4468, remainder rates 1.2426 / 1.0672 / 0.9165), superseded by Line N5 (N5 v2 refereed READY in lineN6; its packet is integrated with Lines N5-N19b, mathematics/fixed-map/lineN5N18_gas_conditional.md; no N5 statement is registered except the threshold T' = 1.42785 used in H7*, BGS-TRACE-305); the 16D = 1 dominant-family identification and its [N] shares (88.9% / 62.0%). |

The H-product route is vacuous (N15 section 5). Vector-chain conversion per window: E/\|r\| ~ 2e-3 for singles, ~ 8% (median) for pair windows, max 5.27 [P-sketch lemma, which must include the second-order dPi^2 \|\|Q\|\| term; CA-fp numerics], giving delta_true(G <= 15) <= 2.470e-6 (BGS-TRACE-322). Under box widening the G > 15 bound is flat (~1.5e-7 per G), so it is not controlled; the pair-window error is dominated (~93%) by the accumulated per-step core -> true perturbation, so a difference-form conversion must cancel that term, not only the end-projector term (N16 section 2; lineN16 section 1); single-core conversion <= 2.094e-8 relative [CA]+[P-sketch]. H4 met (THEOREM_DRAFT_v5, 1 November 2026): the closure condition ("A difference-form (pair-minus-product) core -> true conversion cancelling the per-step perturbation (dominant) and end-projector terms, and a bound on the G > 15 true-functional tail") is met by N21 v2 [P] + [CA-fp] (not [CA]): Lemma D^T (BGS-TRACE-327, difference form, G >= 37 tail <= 1.0843e-7, G > 60 <= 3.1e-13) with Lemma Q (BGS-TRACE-328), Lemma C (BGS-TRACE-335, delta_true(G <= 15) <= 2.2102e-6) and the all-G bound delta_true <= 5.0313e-6 (BGS-TRACE-326), refereed lineN21, lineN21b and lineN22. The vector-chain description above is the superseded N16 route (defects F21-1/F21-3, lineN21). Standing-package inputs are taken as certified as for BGS-TRACE-307 (N8 n_cert constants, N13 V1-V3b, Line F F2, N6 symmetry). |

Gas notation (THEOREM_DRAFT_v3 section 1): level-h_p sum phi^-1 Dev_{h_p} >= S_rho (n/h_p)(\|1 + X + Y\|^2 - 1), S_rho = phi^-1 \|Z_rho\|^2 / h_p; X the isotropic sum (at composite h_p also the 2^omega(h_p) - 2 cross lines, not coded by the gas), Y = Y_U + Y_mix + partially isotropic points; gas X_coded = 2[(1 + Lambda)^p e^{p Delta} - 1] - (dense part) (N11 section 4); delta = interaction constant; Re Lambda_lo' = 6.234e-4. [P-arith: float/mpmath evaluation + monotonicity] (N19b a_crossover_v3.py, robin() corrected; v2 in N18b had the bug, to the Robin term 0.6483/ln ln K_p; REPORT_N17b_v3; referee mpmath recomputation with exact K_p, lineN16 section 2a: margins 0.0153 at p = 1386 and 0.0021 at p = 1390, no violation at any prime 1399 <= p <= 5000, Robin 12.849 at p = 1289). Monotonicity [P]: G'(p) = 4x(2x - 1) ln b increasing, envelope ln K_p <= (2p+1) ln eps, slope bound 2e^gamma/(2p+1) < 0.0219 (Lean Bgs.LineN16.env_slope_small). Scope: the stated idealisation only. This claim is an arithmetic implication in the input delta: for delta = 2.470e-6 the inequality holds for all p >= 1390, and since the gas term 2(1 + Re Lambda_lo')^p e^{-p delta} decreases in delta, also for every delta <= 2.470e-6. Whether delta_true(G <= 15) <= 2.470e-6 actually holds is BGS-TRACE-322 (proved a fortiori since 1 November 2026 via BGS-TRACE-335: delta_true(G <= 15) <= 2.2102e-6); this claim neither asserts nor depends on it. 1390 is also only a lower bound for the true p1, because delta in B_p is the all-G true delta whose G > 15 part is open (H4); p1 with all budgets is not computed. Superseded: N18b's 1382/1386 (robin() used 0.6483/ln K_p) and 'about 1500 with v1 budgets'. Boundary (THEOREM_DRAFT_v5, referee lineN22): the values 1386/1390/1399 charge delta only (A_bad^KP = 0). They are NOT the theorem's p1: B_p carries e^{-p(delta + A_bad^KP)} (THEOREM_DRAFT_v5 section 2). With all rate budgets see BGS-TRACE-333: first prime 3307 (H1 at C <= 0.082) / 1409 (C <= 8.8e-4). At delta_true = 5.0313e-6 alone: 1397/1399 (N21 v2; referee lineN21 section 4). This claim remains a true arithmetic implication in the input delta. |

| `BGS-TRACE-337` | **PROVED** | Span theorem (Theorem A): for every nonzero alpha in Z[sqrt2], log_eps(\|N(alpha)\|/2) + 1 < span(alpha) < log_eps(\|N(alpha)\|/2) + 3; all balanced eps-digits lie in {-1, 0, 1}; span = K_R - K_L + 3 except on the class of sqrt2 (span 2) | [source](../mathematics/fixed-map/lineN_span_theorem.md) | Setting: alpha = a + b sqrt2 in Z[sqrt2] \ {0}, eps = 1 + sqrt2, lambda = eps^2, N(alpha) = a^2 - 2b^2, shell j: 2^j <= \|N\| < 2^{j+1}; mu = (sqrt2/4) alpha, theta_k = sigma+(mu) eps^k, conj theta_k = sigma-(mu) conj(eps)^k, theta_k + conj theta_k = T_k in Z, \|theta_k conj theta_k\| = \|N\|/8; red(x) = x - floor(x + 1/2) in [-1/2, 1/2), n_k = floor(theta_k + 1/2), E_k = n_k - 2 n_{k-1} - n_{k-2}; span = length of the word from the first to the last nonzero E_k; L(alpha) = log_eps(\|N\|/2); c_eps = log 2 / log eps = 0.786440 (0.7864397013573949). [P] (PR #85 span_law.md Lemmas 1-6 for the statement, the exact formula and the lower bound; upper bound by the two-lift argument of P2 Lemma S, with the PR #83 floor-form derivation recorded as a secondary proof after its slips were fixed: red range [-1/2, 1/2), the comparison with 2j via 8 < 32 < eps^4 = 17 + 12 sqrt2 (225 < 288), equality of the norm form floor(L) + 3 at alpha = 2, shell form floor(c_eps j) + 3 attained first in shell 2 by 3 + sqrt2). K_L = min{k : \|theta_k\| >= 1/2}, K_R = max{k : \|conj theta_k\| >= 1/2}. The span is a class invariant (alpha -> +-eps^k alpha). Corollary B (near-sharpness only): neither constant 1 nor 3 can be improved by more than 3.4e-4 (witnesses 82 sqrt2 and 116, BGS-TRACE-339); it is NOT claimed that 1 and 3 are the best constants. Corollary C (shell form): span < c_eps j + 3, so span <= floor(c_eps j) + 3 for j >= 1 and <= 2j for j >= 2. Corollary D: the Prop. K exclusion length span + g0 < c_eps j + 3 + g0 for every class in shell j (replaces the N20 [P-sketch] span <= 2j for j >= 5; prior idea: N20 section 3.4 sketch span <= 0.787 j + O(1)). Finite check [exact integers, not part of the proof]: all 81709 fundamental-domain classes through shell 16 (span_census.py), span - floor(L) histogram {2: 43304, 3: 38405}, 0 letters outside {-1,0,1}, 0 failures of the exact formula or of the strict two-sided bound. | `BGS-TRACE-348` | **PROVED** | Lemma W sharpened [P]: for an odd prime p and a prime q \| h_p = (eps^p + epsbar^p)/2, eps = 1 + sqrt2: q = 1 (mod 2p) and q = +-1 (mod 8); the mod-8 part holds for every odd index n | [source](../computations/verification/h5_ceiling/REPORT_H5_CEILING.md) | Convention (BGS-TRACE-342, Bgs.H6LemmaW): eps = 1 + sqrt2, epsbar = 1 - sqrt2, lambda = eps^2 = 3 + 2 sqrt2, eps^n = a_n + b_n sqrt2, h_n = (eps^n + epsbar^n)/2 = a_n (h_0 = h_1 = 1, h_{n+2} = 2 h_{n+1} + h_n; h_3 = 7, h_5 = 41, h_11 = 23 * 353); for odd p, h_p = u_{(p-1)/2} with u_0 = 1, u_1 = 7, u_{n+1} = 6 u_n - u_{n-1} (BGS-TRACE-344); Lean Bgs.H5CeilingArith.a_eq_h6 identifies a_n with Bgs.H6LemmaW.h n for every n. [P] (REPORT_H5_CEILING section 1): h_n is odd, so q is odd; a_p^2 - 2 b_p^2 = -1 gives 2 b_p^2 = 1 (mod q), so s = 2 b_p is a square root of 2 mod q and q = +-1 (mod 8) (uses only p odd); for any such s, e = 1 + s is a unit (e(1 - s) = -1), e^p = b_p s, e^{2p} = 2 b_p^2 = 1 and e^2 - 1 = 2e != 0, so e^2 has order exactly p in F_q^*, p \| q - 1 and, q and p odd, 2p \| q - 1. Sharpens BGS-TRACE-342 (q = +-1 mod 2p; not edited, remains true): the inert branch of its proof never occurs because 2 is always a square mod q. [CA] (h5_arith.py, exact; no residue-class presieve): every prime q < 10^7 against every odd prime p <= 1500, 207 hits, each re-confirmed with the exact big integer h_p, 0 violations of q = 1 (mod 2p) and of q = +-1 (mod 8); complete factorisations for odd primes p <= 61 (sympy; factors above 2^64 are BPSW probable primes) satisfy both congruences and are squarefree. Lean (Bgs.H5CeilingArith.prime_dvd_mod2p, prime_dvd_mod8, orderOf_lambda; Mathlib; no sorry, no native_decide; axioms propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound): general proof for every odd prime p, every prime q and every odd n (mod 8). Does not prove that h_p is squarefree. Elementary arithmetic of the integers h_p only: no bound on any class sum; H5 (BGS-TRACE-303) stays open, Theorem R#-mod v5 (BGS-TRACE-325) stays conditional, nothing is claimed about R' (BGS-TRACE-206). |

| `BGS-TRACE-350` | **PROVED** | Sophie Germain family [P]: if p = 3 (mod 4) and 2p + 1 is prime then (2p + 1) \| h_p, the least prime factor of h_p is exactly 2p + 1 and h_p is composite for p >= 5; conversely, for odd p with 2p + 1 prime, (2p + 1) \| h_p forces p = 3 (mod 4); 25 such p in [5, 1500], exactly the p there with least prime factor 2p + 1 | [source](../computations/verification/h5_ceiling/REPORT_H5_CEILING.md) | Convention (BGS-TRACE-342, Bgs.H6LemmaW): eps = 1 + sqrt2, epsbar = 1 - sqrt2, lambda = eps^2 = 3 + 2 sqrt2, eps^n = a_n + b_n sqrt2, h_n = (eps^n + epsbar^n)/2 = a_n (h_0 = h_1 = 1, h_{n+2} = 2 h_{n+1} + h_n; h_3 = 7, h_5 = 41, h_11 = 23 * 353); for odd p, h_p = u_{(p-1)/2} with u_0 = 1, u_1 = 7, u_{n+1} = 6 u_n - u_{n-1} (BGS-TRACE-344); Lean Bgs.H5CeilingArith.a_eq_h6 identifies a_n with Bgs.H6LemmaW.h n for every n. [P] (REPORT_H5_CEILING section 3): (i) q = 2n + 1 = 7 (mod 8), so 2 has a square root s mod q and e = 1 + s is a unit; Fermat gives e^{2n} = 1; with A = a_n, B = b_n and 2B^2 = A^2 + 1, e^{2n} - 1 = 2A e^n (the image of lambda^n - 1 = 2 h_n eps^n), so A = 0 mod q; this needs only n = 3 (mod 4), not n prime. (ii) by the mod-8 part of BGS-TRACE-348, an odd n = 1 (mod 4) gives q = 3 (mod 8), impossible. (iii) every prime factor of h_p is = 1 (mod 2p), hence >= 2p + 1; h_p >= 2^{p-1} > 2p + 1 for p >= 5. [CA] (h5_arith.py, exact): for every odd prime p <= 1500, (2p+1) \| h_p iff (p = 3 mod 4 and 2p+1 prime), no mismatch; trial division of h_p by all primes <= 2p + 1 finds no divisor other than 2p + 1; for 5 <= p <= 1500 the family is {11, 23, 83, 131, 179, 191, 239, 251, 359, 419, 431, 443, 491, 659, 683, 719, 743, 911, 1019, 1031, 1103, 1223, 1439, 1451, 1499} (25), equal to the set of p with least prime factor 2p + 1. | `BGS-TRACE-352` | **PROVED** | Lemma S on cross lines [P]: if abar = abar_+ abar_- (coprime, odd norms d_+, d_- > 1), eps^p = +1 mod abar_+ and -1 mod abar_-, and sigma is the side with d_sigma = max(d_+, d_-), then G(c eps^n) + G(c eps^{n+p}) >= sqrt(max(d_+, d_-)) for every n and every class c not in abar_{-sigma}; on the axis sub-line 0 != c in abar_{-sigma} only G >= sqrt(min(d_+, d_-)) holds; the consequence max(d_+, d_-) >= sqrt(h_p) uses squarefree h_p and is conditional on H6 (BGS-TRACE-304) | [source](../computations/verification/lemma_s/REPORT_LEMMA_S.md) | Convention (BGS-TRACE-342/348): eps = 1 + sqrt2, epsbar = 1 - sqrt2 = -1/eps, lambda = eps^2, O = Z[sqrt2], h_p = (eps^p + epsbar^p)/2 for an odd prime p (h_11 = 23 * 353, h_13 = 79 * 599, h_17 = 103 * 15607). Line setting (REPORT_LEMMA_S section 0; grok47 h5_xline): a line is an ideal abar of O with O/abar = Z/h_p and lambda^p = 1 mod abar; eps^p = s_q in {+1, -1} mod each prime q of abar; abar_+- = product of the primes with s_q = +-1, d_+- = N(abar_+-); Binet line iff min(d_+, d_-) = 1, cross line otherwise; if h_p is squarefree (H6, BGS-TRACE-304, open) the lines are the 2^omega ideals of norm h_p with one prime above each q \| h_p and d_+ d_- = h_p. Height G(x) = min{max(\|rho\|, \|rho'\|) : rho = x mod abar} = min{\|a\| + sqrt2 \|b\| : a + b sqrt2 = x mod abar}. [P] (REPORT_LEMMA_S section 1; research1105b REPORT section 4.2, re-derived by the referee, item 1): minimal representatives rho_1 = c eps^n, rho_2 = c eps^{n+p} satisfy abar_+ \| rho_2 - rho_1 and abar_- \| rho_2 + rho_1; rho_2 - rho_1 = -2 c eps^n != 0 mod abar_- when c is not in abar_- (2, eps units at odd primes), so d_+ N(gcd(c, abar_-)) <= \|N(rho_2 - rho_1)\| <= (G_1 + G_2)^2, and symmetrically; general form (G_1 + G_2)^2 >= d_+ N(gcd(c, abar_-)) whenever c is not in abar_-, and with + and - exchanged. Scope caveat (referee): the classes 0 != c in abar_{-sigma} form a Binet-type sub-line of level d_sigma (eps^p acts by the single sign sigma, G(c eps^{n+p}) = G(c eps^n)) on which only G >= sqrt(min(d_+, d_-)) holds; so the source's 'no hypothesis on P^-(h_p) is needed for the vacuum part' holds only off this axis sub-line, where the smallest-prime ceiling returns through sqrt(min(d_+, d_-)). Conditional consequence: with h_p squarefree (H6, BGS-TRACE-304, open) d_+ d_- = h_p, hence max(d_+, d_-) >= sqrt(h_p) and G_1 + G_2 >= h_p^{1/4} off the axis sub-line; without H6 this is not claimed. [CA] (lemmaS_exact.py, exhaustive over all h classes of every line, heights in float64 with margins >= 3%): p = 11, 13, 17 (h = 23 * 353, 79 * 599, 103 * 15607; omega = 2, 2 cross lines each): the bound holds for every class outside abar_{-sigma} and the gcd form on every class; Q = min over primitive x of max(G(x), G(x eps^p)) = 15.2426, 21.7990, 68 and Q/sqrt(d_sigma) = 0.8113, 0.8907, 0.5443; min over primitive x of (G_1 + G_2)/sqrt(d_sigma) = 1.1618, 1.3261, 1.0345 (within 3.5% of equality at p = 17); axis sub-line minima of G = 6.414, 10.414, 15.243 against sqrt(min) = 4.796, 8.888, 10.149; Binet lines Q = 1. omega >= 3 (first case p = 37) is not tested; the proof does not depend on omega. No bound on X_line, X_cross or any class sum is claimed; the seam-decay threshold r < 0.418, the seam-cost and counting inputs and every [N]/[H]/[C] item of the source are not registered. H5 (BGS-TRACE-303) stays open, H6 (BGS-TRACE-304) stays open, Theorem R#-mod v5 (BGS-TRACE-325) stays conditional, nothing is claimed about R' (BGS-TRACE-206). |
